            '                        count: visibleFocusItems.length,\n'
            '                      ),\n'
            '                      const SizedBox(height: 8),\n'
            '                      if (focusItems.isEmpty)\n'
            '                        Padding(\n'
            '                          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 18),\n'
            '                          child: Text(\n'
            '                            \'暂无进行中的问题\',\n'
            '                            style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium,\n'
            '                          ),\n'
            '                        )\n'
            '                      else ...[\n'
            '                        for (var i = 0; i < visibleFocusItems.length; i++) ...[\n'
            '                          _FocusRow(\n'
            '                            item: visibleFocusItems[i],\n'
            '                            onTap: () =>\n'
            '                                widget.onOpenCase(visibleFocusItems[i]),\n'
            '                          ),\n'
            '                          if (i < visibleFocusItems.length - 1)\n'
            '                            Divider(height: 1, color: scheme.outlineVariant),\n'
            '                        ],\n'
            '                        if (hasAdditionalFocusItems) ...[\n'
            '                          const SizedBox(height: 6),\n'
            '                          TextButton.icon(\n'
            '                            key: const Key(\'v2-student-focus-toggle\'),\n'
            '                            onPressed: () => setState(\n'
            '                              () => _showAllFocusItems = !_showAllFocusItems,\n'
            '                            ),\n'
            '                            icon: Icon(\n'
            '                              _showAllFocusItems\n'
            '                                  ? Icons.expand_less\n'
            '                                  : Icons.expand_more,\n'
            '                              size: 18,\n'
            '                            ),\n'
            '                            label: Text(\n'
            '                              _showAllFocusItems\n'
            '                                  ? \'只看重点\'\n'
            '                                  : \'查看全部 ${focusItems.length} 个\',\n'
            '                            ),\n'
            '                          ),\n'
            '                        ],\n'
            '                      ],\n'
            '                      if (closedItems.isNotEmpty) ...[\n'
            '                        const SizedBox(height: 34),\n'
            '                        _SectionTitle(title: \'历史问题\', count: closedItems.length),\n'
            '                        const SizedBox(height: 8),\n'
            '                        for (var i = 0; i < closedItems.length; i++) ...[\n'
            '                          _FocusRow(\n'
            '                            item: closedItems[i],\n'
            '                            onTap: () => widget.onOpenCase(closedItems[i]),\n'
            '                          ),\n'
            '                          if (i < closedItems.length - 1)\n'
            '                            Divider(height: 1, color: scheme.outlineVariant),\n'
            '                        ],\n'
            '                      ],\n'
            '                      const SizedBox(height: 34),\n'
            '                      const _SectionTitle(title: \'最近成长\'),\n'
            '                      const SizedBox(height: 14),\n'
            '                      _Timeline(entries: timelineEntries),\n'
            '                    ],\n'
            '                  ),\n'
            '                ),\n'
            '              ),\n'
            '            ),\n'
            '          ),\n'
            '        ],\n'
            '      ),\n'
            '    );\n'
            '  }\n'
            '}\n'
            '\n'
            'class _StudentHeader extends StatelessWidget {\n'
            '  const _StudentHeader({required this.student, required this.compact});\n'
            '\n'
            '  final V2Student student;\n'
            '  final bool compact;\n'
            '\n'
            '  @override\n'
            '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
            '    final data = V2WorkspaceDataScope.of(context);\n'
            '    final focusItems = data.focusItemsForStudent(student);\n'
            '    final exportStudent = _V2RuntimeScope.maybeOf(context)?.studentExport;\n'
            '    final buttons = Wrap(\n'
            '      spacing: 8,\n'
            '      runSpacing: 8,\n'
            '      children: [\n'
            '        OutlinedButton.icon(\n'
            '          onPressed: () => _showV2QuickCaptureForStudent(context, student),\n'
            '          icon: const Icon(Icons.note_add_outlined, size: 18),\n'
            '          label: const Text(\'记录问题\'),\n'
            '        ),\n'
            '        FilledButton.icon(\n'
            '          onPressed: focusItems.isEmpty\n'
            '              ? null\n'
            '              : () => _showV2ProgressCasePicker(context, student),\n'
            '          icon: const Icon(Icons.edit_note_outlined, size: 18),\n'
            '          label: const Text(\'记进展\'),\n'
            '        ),\n'
            '        if (exportStudent != null)\n'
            '          PopupMenuButton<String>(\n'
            '            key: const Key(\'v2-student-more-actions\'),\n'
            '            tooltip: \'更多操作\',\n'
            '            icon: const Icon(Icons.more_horiz),\n'
            '            onSelected: (value) async {\n'
            '              if (value == \'export\') {\n'
            '                await exportStudent(context, student);\n'
            '              }\n'
            '            },\n'
            '            itemBuilder: (_) => const [\n'
            '              PopupMenuItem<String>(\n'
            '                value: \'export\',\n'
            '                child: ListTile(\n'
            '                  leading: Icon(Icons.download_outlined),\n'
            '                  title: Text(\'导出学情记录\'),\n'
            '                  contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,\n'
            '                ),\n'
            '              ),\n'
            '            ],\n'
            '          ),\n'
            '      ],\n'
            '    );\n'
            '    return Column(\n'
            '      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
            '      children: [\n'
            '        Text(\n'
            '          \'学生 · ${student.name}\',\n'
            '          style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall,\n'
            '        ),\n'
            '        const SizedBox(height: 18),\n'
            '        if (compact) ...[\n'
            '          Text(student.name, style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.headlineSmall),\n'
            '          const SizedBox(height: 5),\n'
            '          Text(\n'
            '            \'${student.grade} · ${student.subjects.join(\' / \')}\',\n'
            '            style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyLarge,\n'
            '          ),\n'
            '          if (student.teacherSummary.trim().isNotEmpty) ...[\n'
            '            const SizedBox(height: 5),\n'
            '            Text(\n'
            '              student.teacherSummary,\n'
            '              style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall,\n'
            '            ),\n'
            '          ],\n'
            '          const SizedBox(height: 16),\n'
            '          buttons,\n'
            '        ] else\n'
            '          Row(\n'
            '            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
            '            children: [\n'
            '              Expanded(\n'
            '                child: Column(\n'
            '                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
            '                  children: [\n'
            '                    Text(\n'
            '                      student.name,\n'
            '                      style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.headlineSmall,\n'
            '                    ),\n'
            '                    const SizedBox(height: 5),\n'
            '                    Text(\n'
            '                      \'${student.grade} · ${student.subjects.join(\' / \')}\',\n'
            '                      style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyLarge,\n'
            '                    ),\n'
            '                    if (student.teacherSummary.trim().isNotEmpty) ...[\n'
            '                      const SizedBox(height: 5),\n'
            '                      Text(\n'
            '                        student.teacherSummary,\n'
            '                        style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall,\n'
            '                      ),\n'
            '                    ],\n'
            '                  ],\n'
            '                ),\n'
            '              ),\n'
            '              buttons,\n'
            '            ],\n'
            '          ),\n'
            '      ],\n'
            '    );\n'
            '  }\n'
            '}\n'
            '\n'
            'class _SectionTitle extends StatelessWidget {\n'
            '  const _SectionTitle({required this.title, this.count});\n'
            '\n'
            '  final String title;\n'
            '  final int? count;\n'
            '\n'
            '  @override\n'
            '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
            '    return Row(\n'
            '      children: [\n'
            '        Text(title, style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleLarge),\n'
            '        if (count != null) ...[\n'
            '          const SizedBox(width: 8),\n'
            '          Text(\'$count\', style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall),\n'
            '        ],\n'
            '      ],\n'
            '    );\n'
            '  }\n'
            '}\n'
            '\n'
            'class _FocusRow extends StatelessWidget {\n'
            '  const _FocusRow({required this.item, required this.onTap, this.studentName});\n'
            '\n'
            '  final V2FocusItem item;\n'
            '  final VoidCallback onTap;\n'
            '  final String? studentName;\n'
            '\n'
            '  @override\n'
            '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
            '    final scheme = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;\n'
            '    return InkWell(\n'
            '      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),\n'
            '      onTap: onTap,\n'
            '      child: Padding(\n'
            '        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16),\n'
            '        child: Row(\n'
            '          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
            '          children: [\n'
            '            Container(\n'
            '              width: 2,\n'
            '              height: 58,\n'
            '              color: scheme.primary.withValues(alpha: 0.5),\n'
            '            ),\n'
            '            const SizedBox(width: 16),\n'
            '            Expanded(\n'
            '              child: Column(\n'
            '                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
            '                children: [\n'
            '                  Text(\n'
            '                    item.title,\n'
            '                    maxLines: 2,\n'
            '                    overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,\n'
            '                    style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleMedium,\n'
            '                  ),\n'
            '                  if (studentName != null) ...[\n'
            '                    const SizedBox(height: 4),\n'
            '                    Text(\n'
            '                      \'$studentName · ${item.subject}\',\n'
            '                      style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall,\n'
            '                    ),\n'
            '                  ],\n'
            '                  if (studentName == null && _shouldShowCaseSummary(item)) ...[\n'
            '                    const SizedBox(height: 4),\n'
            '                    Text(\n'
            '                      item.summary,\n'
            '                      style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium,\n'
            '                    ),\n'
            '                  ],\n'
            '                  const SizedBox(height: 8),\n'
            '                  Text(\n'
            '                    item.closed\n'
            '                        ? _displayNextStep(item.nextStep)\n'
            '                        : \'下一步  ${_displayNextStep(item.nextStep)}\',\n'
            '                    maxLines: 2,\n'
            '                    overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,\n'
            '                    style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall,\n'
            '                  ),\n'
            '                ],\n'
            '              ),\n'
            '            ),\n'
            '            if (studentName == null) ...[\n'
            '              const SizedBox(width: 16),\n'
            '              Column(\n'
            '                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end,\n'
            '                children: [\n'
            '                  Text(\n'
            '                    item.subject,\n'
            '                    style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall,\n'
            '                  ),\n'
            '                  const SizedBox(height: 17),\n'
            '                  Text(\n'
            '                    item.dueLabel,\n'
            '                    style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall,\n'
            '                  ),\n'
            '                ],\n'
            '              ),\n'
            '            ] else if (!item.closed &&\n'
            '                item.dueLabel.trim().isNotEmpty &&\n'
            '                item.dueLabel != \'待安排\') ...[\n'
            '              const SizedBox(width: 12),\n'
            '              Text(item.dueLabel, style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall),\n'
            '            ],\n'
            '            const SizedBox(width: 4),\n'
            '            const Icon(Icons.chevron_right, size: 20),\n'
            '          ],\n'
            '        ),\n'
            '      ),\n'
            '    );\n'
            '  }\n'
            '}\n'
            '\n'
            'class _Timeline extends StatelessWidget {\n'
            '  const _Timeline({required this.entries, this.resolveEvidencePhotos = false});\n'
            '\n'
            '  final List<V2TimelineEntry> entries;\n'
            '  final bool resolveEvidencePhotos;\n'
            '\n'
            '  @override\n'
            '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
            '    if (entries.isEmpty) {\n'
            '      return Text(\'暂时还没有成长记录\', style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium);\n'
            '    }\n'
            '    return Column(\n'
            '      children: [\n'
            '        for (var i = 0; i < entries.length; i++)\n'
            '          _TimelineRow(\n'
            '            entry: entries[i],\n'
            '            last: i == entries.length - 1,\n'
            '            resolveEvidencePhotos: resolveEvidencePhotos,\n'
            '          ),\n'
            '      ],\n'
            '    );\n'
            '  }\n'
            '}\n'
            '\n'
            'class _TimelineRow extends StatelessWidget {\n'
            '  const _TimelineRow({\n'
            '    required this.entry,\n'
            '    required this.last,\n'
            '    required this.resolveEvidencePhotos,\n'
            '  });\n'
            '\n'
            '  final V2TimelineEntry entry;\n'
            '  final bool last;\n'
            '  final bool resolveEvidencePhotos;\n'
            '\n'
            '  @override\n'
            '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
            '    final scheme = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;\n'
            '    final attachmentRepository = _V2RuntimeScope.maybeOf(context)\n'
            '        ?.evidenceAttachmentRepository;\n'
            '    final canResolveRealPhotos =\n'
            '        resolveEvidencePhotos &&\n'
            '        entry.evidenceId != null &&\n'
            '        attachmentRepository != null;\n'
            '    return Row(\n'
            '      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
            '      children: [\n'
            '        SizedBox(\n'
            '          width: 74,\n'
            '          child: Padding(\n'
            '            padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 1),\n'
            '            child: Text(\n'
            '              entry.date,\n'
            '              style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall,\n'
            '            ),\n'
            '          ),\n'
            '        ),\n'
            '        SizedBox(\n'
            '          width: 24,\n'
            '          child: Column(\n'
            '            children: [\n'
            '              Container(\n'
            '                width: 7,\n'
            '                height: 7,\n'
            '                margin: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 7),\n'
            '                decoration: BoxDecoration(\n'
            '                  color: entry.kind == \'新表现\' ? scheme.primary : scheme.outline,\n'
            '                  shape: BoxShape.circle,\n'
            '                ),\n'
            '              ),\n'
            '              if (!last)\n'
            '                Container(\n'
            '                  width: 1,\n'
            '                  height: entry.photoCount > 0 || canResolveRealPhotos\n'
            '                      ? 144\n'
            '                      : 96,\n'
            '                  color: scheme.outlineVariant,\n'
            '                ),\n'
            '            ],\n'
            '          ),\n'
            '        ),\n'
            '        const SizedBox(width: 10),\n'
            '        Expanded(\n'
            '          child: Padding(\n'
            '            padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 24),\n'
            '            child: Column(\n'
            '              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
            '              children: [\n'
            '                Text(\n'
            '                  entry.kind,\n'
            '                  style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleMedium,\n'
            '                ),\n'
            '                const SizedBox(height: 5),\n'
            '                Text(entry.body, style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium),\n'
            '                if (canResolveRealPhotos) ...[\n'
            '                  const SizedBox(height: 12),\n'
            '                  _EvidencePhotoStrip(\n'
            '                    evidenceId: entry.evidenceId!,\n'
            '                    repository: attachmentRepository,\n'
            '                  ),\n'
            '                ] else if (entry.photoCount > 0) ...[\n'
            '                  const SizedBox(height: 12),\n'
            '                  _PhotoStrip(count: entry.photoCount),\n'
            '                ],\n'
            '                const SizedBox(height: 9),\n'
            '                Text(\n'
            '                  entry.teacher.trim().isEmpty\n'
            '                      ? entry.time\n'
            '                      : \'${entry.teacher} · ${entry.time}\',\n'
            '                  style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall,\n'
            '                ),\n'
            '              ],\n'
            '            ),\n'
            '          ),\n'
            '        ),\n'
            '      ],\n'
            '    );\n'
            '  }\n'
            '}\n'
            '\n'
            'Future<void> _showV2EvidencePhotoPreview(\n'
            '  BuildContext context,\n'
            '  String signedUrl,\n'
            ') {\n'
            '  final compact = MediaQuery.sizeOf(context).width < 720;\n'
            '  return showDialog<void>(\n'
            '    context: context,\n'
            '    barrierColor: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.86),\n'
            '    builder: (dialogContext) => Dialog(\n'
            '      insetPadding: EdgeInsets.all(compact ? 12 : 40),\n'
            '      clipBehavior: Clip.antiAlias,\n'
            '      child: SizedBox(\n'
            '        width: compact ? double.maxFinite : 900,\n'
            '        height: compact ? MediaQuery.sizeOf(dialogContext).height * 0.82 : 680,\n'
            '        child: Stack(\n'
            '          children: [\n'
            '            Positioned.fill(\n'
            '              child: ColoredBox(\n'
            '                color: Theme.of(dialogContext).colorScheme.surface,\n'
            '                child: Center(\n'
            '                  child: InteractiveViewer(\n'
            '                    minScale: 0.5,\n'
            '                    maxScale: 5,\n'
            '                    child: Image.network(\n'
            '                      signedUrl,\n'
            '                      key: const Key(\'v2-evidence-photo-preview-image\'),\n'
            '                      fit: BoxFit.contain,\n'
            '                      loadingBuilder: (context, child, progress) {\n'
            '                        if (progress == null) return child;\n'
            '                        final expected = progress.expectedTotalBytes;\n'
            '                        final value = expected == null || expected <= 0\n'
            '                            ? null\n'
            '                            : progress.cumulativeBytesLoaded / expected;\n'
            '                        return Center(\n'
            '                          child: SizedBox(\n'
            '                            width: 34,\n'
            '                            height: 34,\n'
            '                            child: CircularProgressIndicator(value: value),\n'
            '                          ),\n'
            '                        );\n'
            '                      },\n'
            '                      errorBuilder: (_, _, _) => Padding(\n'
            '                        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(32),\n'
            '                        child: Column(\n'
            '                          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,\n'
            '                          children: [\n'
            '                            const Icon(Icons.broken_image_outlined, size: 36),\n'
            '                            const SizedBox(height: 12),\n'
            '                            Text(\n'
            '                              \'图片暂时无法打开，请返回后重试。\',\n'
            '                              textAlign: TextAlign.center,\n'
            '                              style: Theme.of(dialogContext)\n'
            '                                  .textTheme\n'
            '                                  .bodyMedium,\n'
            '                            ),\n'
            '                          ],\n'
            '                        ),\n'
            '                      ),\n'
            '                    ),\n'
            '                  ),\n'
            '                ),\n'
            '              ),\n'
            '            ),\n'
            '            Positioned(\n'
            '              top: 8,\n'
            '              right: 8,\n'
            '              child: Material(\n'
            '                color: Theme.of(dialogContext)\n'
            '                    .colorScheme\n'
            '                    .surfaceContainerHighest,\n'
            '                shape: const CircleBorder(),\n'
            '                child: IconButton(\n'
            '                  key: const Key(\'v2-evidence-photo-preview-close\'),\n'
            '                  tooltip: \'关闭图片\',\n'
            '                  onPressed: () => Navigator.of(dialogContext).pop(),\n'
            '                  icon: const Icon(Icons.close),\n'
            '                ),\n'
            '              ),\n'
            '            ),\n'
            '          ],\n'
            '        ),\n'
            '      ),\n'
            '    ),\n'
            '  );\n'
            '}\n'
            '\n'
            'class _EvidencePhotoStrip extends StatefulWidget {\n'
            '  const _EvidencePhotoStrip({\n'
            '    required this.evidenceId,\n'
            '    required this.repository,\n'
            '  });\n'
            '\n'
            '  final String evidenceId;\n'
            '  final EvidenceAttachmentRepository repository;\n'
            '\n'
            '  @override\n'
            '  State<_EvidencePhotoStrip> createState() => _EvidencePhotoStripState();\n'
            '}\n'
            '\n'
            'class _EvidencePhotoStripState extends State<_EvidencePhotoStrip> {\n'
            '  late Future<List<String>> _signedUrls;\n'
            '\n'
            '  @override\n'
            '  void initState() {\n'
            '    super.initState();\n'
            '    _signedUrls = _loadSignedUrls();\n'
            '  }\n'
            '\n'
            '  @override\n'
            '  void didUpdateWidget(covariant _EvidencePhotoStrip oldWidget) {\n'
            '    super.didUpdateWidget(oldWidget);\n'
            '    if (oldWidget.evidenceId != widget.evidenceId ||\n'
            '        oldWidget.repository != widget.repository) {\n'
            '      _signedUrls = _loadSignedUrls();\n'
            '    }\n'
            '  }\n'
            '\n'
            '  Future<List<String>> _loadSignedUrls() async {\n'
            '    final attachments = await widget.repository.listForEvidence(\n'
            '      widget.evidenceId,\n'
            '    );\n'
            '    final result = <String>[];\n'
            '    for (final attachment in attachments.take(3)) {\n'
            '      result.add(\n'
            '        await widget.repository.createSignedUrl(attachment.storagePath),\n'
            '      );\n'
            '    }\n'
            '    return List<String>.unmodifiable(result);\n'
            '  }\n'
            '\n'
            '  @override\n'
            '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
            '    final scheme = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;\n'
            '    return FutureBuilder<List<String>>(\n'
            '      future: _signedUrls,\n'
            '      builder: (context, snapshot) {\n'
            '        if (snapshot.connectionState != ConnectionState.done) {\n'
            '          return Row(\n'
            '            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,\n'
            '            children: [\n'
            '              const SizedBox(\n'
            '                width: 14,\n'
            '                height: 14,\n'
            '                child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 1.5),\n'
            '              ),\n'
            '              const SizedBox(width: 8),\n'
            '              Text(\'正在加载图片…\', style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall),\n'
            '            ],\n'
            '          );\n'
            '        }\n'
            '        if (snapshot.hasError) {\n'
            '          return Text(\n'
            '            \'图片暂时无法加载\',\n'
            '            style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall\n'
            '                ?.copyWith(color: scheme.onSurfaceVariant),\n'
            '          );\n'
            '        }\n'
            '        final urls = snapshot.data ?? const <String>[];\n'
            '        if (urls.isEmpty) {\n'
            '          return const SizedBox.shrink();\n'
            '        }\n'
            '        return Wrap(\n'
            '          spacing: 8,\n'
            '          runSpacing: 8,\n'
            '          children: [\n'
            '            for (var index = 0; index < urls.length; index++)\n'
            '              Semantics(\n'
            '                button: true,\n'
            '                label: \'查看图片 ${index + 1}\',\n'
            '                child: Material(\n'
            '                  color: Colors.transparent,\n'
            '                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(7),\n'
            '                  child: InkWell(\n'
            '                    key: ValueKey<String>(\'v2-evidence-photo-$index\'),\n'
            '                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(7),\n'
            '                    onTap: () =>\n'
            '                        _showV2EvidencePhotoPreview(context, urls[index]),\n'
            '                    child: ClipRRect(\n'
            '                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(7),\n'
            '                      child: SizedBox(\n'
            '                        width: 96,\n'
            '                        height: 72,\n'
            '                        child: Image.network(\n'
            '                          urls[index],\n'
            '                          fit: BoxFit.cover,\n'
            '                          errorBuilder: (_, _, _) => ColoredBox(\n'
            '                            color: scheme.surfaceContainer,\n'
            '                            child: Icon(\n'
            '                              Icons.broken_image_outlined,\n'
            '                              color: scheme.onSurfaceVariant,\n'
            '                            ),\n'
            '                          ),\n'
            '                        ),\n'
            '                      ),\n'
            '                    ),\n'
            '                  ),\n'
            '                ),\n'
            '              ),\n'
            '          ],\n'
            '        );\n'
            '      },\n'
            '    );\n'
            '  }\n'
            '}\n'
            '\n'
            'class _PhotoStrip extends StatelessWidget {\n'
            '  const _PhotoStrip({required this.count});\n'
            '\n'
            '  final int count;\n'
            '\n'
            '  @override\n'
            '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
            '    final scheme = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;\n'
            '    return Wrap(\n'
            '      spacing: 8,\n'
            '      runSpacing: 8,\n'
            '      children: [\n'
            '        for (var i = 0; i < count.clamp(0, 3); i++)\n'
            '          Container(\n'
            '            width: 86,\n'
            '            height: 66,\n'
            '            decoration: BoxDecoration(\n'
            '              color: scheme.surfaceContainer,\n'
            '              border: Border.all(color: scheme.outlineVariant),\n'
            '              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(7),\n'
            '            ),\n'
            '            child: Icon(\n'
            '              Icons.description_outlined,\n'
            '              color: scheme.onSurfaceVariant.withValues(alpha: 0.7),\n'
            '            ),\n'
            '          ),\n'
            '      ],\n'
            '    );\n'
            '  }\n'
            '}\n'
            '\n'
            'class _CaseDetailPane extends StatelessWidget {\n'
            '  const _CaseDetailPane({\n'
            '    required this.student,\n'
            '    required this.item,\n'
            '    required this.onBack,\n'
            '    this.compact = false,\n'
            '  });\n'
            '\n'
            '  final V2Student student;\n'
            '  final V2FocusItem item;\n'
            '  final VoidCallback onBack;\n'
            '  final bool compact;\n'
            '\n'
            '  @override\n'
            '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
            '    final scheme = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;\n'
            '    final timelineEntries = V2WorkspaceDataScope.of(context)\n'
            '        .timelineForCase(item);\n'
            '    final controller = _V2RuntimeScope.maybeOf(context)?.workflowController;\n'
            '    final pendingAction = item.closed\n'
            '        ? null\n'
            '        : controller?.pendingActionFor(item.id);\n'
            '    final canReopen =\n'
            '        item.closed && (controller?.canReopenClosedCase(item.id) ?? false);\n'
            '    return ColoredBox(\n'
            '      color: scheme.surface,\n'
            '      child: SingleChildScrollView(\n'
            '        child: Center(\n'
            '          child: ConstrainedBox(\n'
            '            constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 860),\n'
            '            child: Padding(\n'
            '              padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(\n'
            '                compact ? 18 : 32,\n'
            '                20,\n'
            '                compact ? 18 : 32,\n'
            '                48,\n'
            '              ),\n'
            '              child: Column(\n'
            '                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
            '                children: [\n'
            '                  IconButton(\n'
            '                    tooltip: \'返回\',\n'
            '                    onPressed: onBack,\n'
            '                    icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back),\n'
            '                  ),\n'
            '                  const SizedBox(height: 8),\n'
            '                  Text(\n'
            '                    \'${student.name} · ${item.subject}\',\n'
            '                    style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall,\n'
            '                  ),\n'
            '                  const SizedBox(height: 12),\n'
            '                  Row(\n'
            '                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
            '                    children: [\n'
            '                      Expanded(\n'
            '                        child: Text(\n'
            '                          item.title,\n'
            '                          style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.headlineSmall,\n'
            '                        ),\n'
            '                      ),\n'
            '                      if (!item.closed) ...[\n'
            '                        const SizedBox(width: 12),\n'
            '                        FilledButton.icon(\n'
            '                          onPressed: () =>\n'
            '                              _showV2ProgressForCase(context, student, item),\n'
            '                          icon: const Icon(Icons.edit_note_outlined, size: 18),\n'
            '                          label: const Text(\'记进展\'),\n'
            '                        ),\n'
            '                        if (controller != null) ...[\n'
            '                          const SizedBox(width: 4),\n'
            '                          PopupMenuButton<String>(\n'
            '                            key: ValueKey<String>(\'v2-case-more-${item.id}\'),\n'
            '                            tooltip: \'更多操作\',\n'
            '                            icon: const Icon(Icons.more_vert),\n'
            '                            onSelected: (value) async {\n'
            '                              if (value != \'delete\') return;\n'
            '                              final removed = await _showV2VoidCase(\n'
            '                                context,\n'
            '                                student,\n'
            '                                item,\n'
            '                              );\n'
            '                              if (removed && context.mounted) onBack();\n'
            '                            },\n'
            '                            itemBuilder: (menuContext) => [\n'
            '                              PopupMenuItem<String>(\n'
            '                                key: ValueKey<String>(\'v2-void-${item.id}\'),\n'
            '                                value: \'delete\',\n'
            '                                child: Row(\n'
            '                                  children: [\n'
            '                                    Icon(\n'
            '                                      Icons.delete_outline,\n'
            '                                      size: 18,\n'
            '                                      color: Theme.of(menuContext)\n'
            '                                          .colorScheme\n'
            '                                          .error,\n'
            '                                    ),\n'
            '                                    const SizedBox(width: 10),\n'
            '                                    Text(\n'
            '                                      \'删除问题\',\n'
            '                                      style: TextStyle(\n'
            '                                        color: Theme.of(menuContext)\n'
            '                                            .colorScheme\n'
            '                                            .error,\n'
            '                                      ),\n'
            '                                    ),\n'
            '                                  ],\n'
            '                                ),\n'
            '                              ),\n'
            '                            ],\n'
            '                          ),\n'
            '                        ],\n'
            '                      ],\n'
            '                    ],\n'
            '                  ),\n'
            '                  if (_shouldShowCaseSummary(item)) ...[\n'
            '                    const SizedBox(height: 8),\n'
            '                    Text(\n'
            '                      item.summary,\n'
            '                      style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyLarge,\n'
            '                    ),\n'
            '                  ],\n'
            '                  const SizedBox(height: 24),\n'
            '                  if (item.closed) ...[\n'
            '                    Text(\'状态\', style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleMedium),\n'
            '                    const SizedBox(height: 6),\n'
            '                    Row(\n'
            '                      children: [\n'
            '                        Icon(\n'
            '                          Icons.check_circle_outline,\n'
            '                          size: 18,\n'
            '                          color: scheme.primary,\n'
            '                        ),\n'
            '                        const SizedBox(width: 8),\n'
            '                        Expanded(\n'
            '                          child: Text(\n'
            '                            _displayNextStep(item.nextStep),\n'
            '                            style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium,\n'
            '                          ),\n'
            '                        ),\n'
            '                      ],\n'
            '                    ),\n'
            '                  ] else\n'
            '                    Container(\n'
            '                      key: ValueKey<String>(\'v2-case-next-step-${item.id}\'),\n'
            '                      width: double.infinity,\n'
            '                      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),\n'
            '                      decoration: BoxDecoration(\n'
            '                        color: scheme.primaryContainer.withValues(alpha: 0.28),\n'
            '                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),\n'
            '                        border: Border.all(\n'
            '                          color: scheme.primary.withValues(alpha: 0.18),\n'
            '                        ),\n'
            '                      ),\n'
            '                      child: Column(\n'
            '                        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
            '                        children: [\n'
            '                          Row(\n'
            '                            children: [\n'
            '                              Expanded(\n'
            '                                child: Text(\n'
            '                                  \'下一步\',\n'
            '                                  style: Theme.of(context)\n'
            '                                      .textTheme\n'
            '                                      .titleMedium,\n'
            '                                ),\n'
            '                              ),\n'
            '                              Text(\n'
            '                                item.dueLabel,\n'
            '                                style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall,\n'
            '                              ),\n'
            '                            ],\n'
            '                          ),\n'
            '                          const SizedBox(height: 8),\n'
            '                          Row(\n'
            '                            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
            '                            children: [\n'
            '                              Padding(\n'
            '                                padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 2),\n'
            '                                child: Icon(\n'
            '                                  Icons.arrow_forward,\n'
            '                                  size: 18,\n'
            '                                  color: scheme.primary,\n'
            '                                ),\n'
            '                              ),\n'
            '                              const SizedBox(width: 8),\n'
            '                              Expanded(\n'
            '                                child: Text(\n'
            '                                  _displayNextStep(item.nextStep),\n'
            '                                  style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyLarge\n'
            '                                      ?.copyWith(fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),\n'
            '                                ),\n'
            '                              ),\n'
            '                            ],\n'
            '                          ),\n'
            '                        ],\n'
            '                      ),\n'
            '                    ),\n'
            '                  if (canReopen) ...[\n'
            '                    const SizedBox(height: 16),\n'
            '                    FilledButton.tonalIcon(\n'
            '                      key: ValueKey<String>(\'v2-reopen-${item.id}\'),\n'
            '                      onPressed: () =>\n'
            '                          _showV2ReopenClosedCase(context, student, item),\n'
            '                      icon: const Icon(Icons.restart_alt, size: 18),\n'
            '                      label: const Text(\'再次出现，重新跟进\'),\n'
            '                    ),\n'
            '                  ],\n'
            '                  if (pendingAction != null) ...[\n'
            '                    const SizedBox(height: 12),\n'
            '                    Wrap(\n'
            '                      spacing: 8,\n'
            '                      runSpacing: 8,\n'
            '                      children: [\n'
            '                        if (pendingAction.canComplete)\n'
            '                          FilledButton.tonalIcon(\n'
            '                            key: ValueKey<String>(\'v2-complete-${item.id}\'),\n'
            '                            onPressed: () => _showV2CompleteCurrentAction(\n'
            '                              context,\n'
            '                              student,\n'
            '                              item,\n'
            '                            ),\n'
            '                            icon: const Icon(\n'
            '                              Icons.check_circle_outline,\n'
            '                              size: 18,\n'
            '                            ),\n'
            '                            label: const Text(\'完成这一步\'),\n'
            '                          ),\n'
            '                        OutlinedButton.icon(\n'
            '                          key: ValueKey<String>(\'v2-reschedule-${item.id}\'),\n'
            '                          onPressed: () => _showV2RescheduleCurrentAction(\n'
            '                            context,\n'
            '                            student,\n'
            '                            item,\n'
            '                          ),\n'
            '                          icon: const Icon(\n'
            '                            Icons.event_repeat_outlined,\n'
            '                            size: 18,\n'
            '                          ),\n'
            '                          label: Text(\n'
            '                            pendingAction.dueOn == null ? \'安排日期\' : \'改期\',\n'
            '                          ),\n'
            '                        ),\n'
            '                      ],\n'
            '                    ),\n'
            '                  ],\n'
            '                  const SizedBox(height: 30),\n'
            '                  Divider(color: scheme.outlineVariant),\n'
            '                  const SizedBox(height: 28),\n'
            '                  const _SectionTitle(title: \'成长过程\'),\n'
            '                  const SizedBox(height: 16),\n'
            '                  _Timeline(\n'
            '                    entries: timelineEntries,\n'
            '                    resolveEvidencePhotos: true,\n'
            '                  ),\n'
            '                ],\n'
            '              ),\n'
            '            ),\n'
            '          ),\n'
            '        ),\n'
            '      ),\n'
            '    );\n'
            '  }\n'
            '}\n'
            '\n'
            'class _TodayPane extends StatelessWidget {\n'
            '  const _TodayPane({\n'
            '    required this.onOpenCase,\n'
            '    required this.onOpenStudent,\n'
            '    this.compact = false,\n'
            '    this.onOpenMore,\n'
            '  });\n'
            '\n'
            '  final ValueChanged<V2FocusItem> onOpenCase;\n'
            '  final ValueChanged<V2Student> onOpenStudent;\n'
            '  final bool compact;\n'
            '  final VoidCallback? onOpenMore;\n'
            '\n'
            '  static int _priority(V2FocusItem item) => switch (item.actionTiming) {\n'
            '    V2ActionTiming.overdue => 0,\n'
            '    V2ActionTiming.today => 1,\n'
            '    V2ActionTiming.undated => 2,\n'
            '    V2ActionTiming.future => 3,\n'
            '    null => 4,\n'
            '  };\n'
            '\n'
            '  static int _compare(V2FocusItem left, V2FocusItem right) {\n'
            '    final priority = _priority(left).compareTo(_priority(right));\n'
            '    if (priority != 0) return priority;\n'
            '    final leftDue = left.dueOn;\n'
            '    final rightDue = right.dueOn;\n'
            '    if (leftDue != null && rightDue != null) {\n'
            '      final due = leftDue.compareTo(rightDue);\n'
            '      if (due != 0) return due;\n'
            '    } else if (leftDue != null) {\n'
            '      return -1;\n'
            '    } else if (rightDue != null) {\n'
            '      return 1;\n'
            '    }\n'
            '    return left.nextStep.compareTo(right.nextStep);\n'
            '  }\n'
            '\n'
            '  @override\n'
            '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
            '    final data = V2WorkspaceDataScope.of(context);\n'
            '    final validItems = data.focusItems\n'
            '        .where(\n'
            '          (item) =>\n'
            '              data.studentForFocusItemOrNull(item) != null &&\n'
            '              item.actionTiming != null,\n'
            '        )\n'
            '        .toList(growable: false);\n'
            '    final currentItems =\n'
            '        validItems\n'
            '            .where(\n'
            '              (item) =>\n'
            '                  item.actionTiming == V2ActionTiming.overdue ||\n'
            '                  item.actionTiming == V2ActionTiming.today,\n'
            '            )\n'
            '            .toList(growable: true)\n'
            '          ..sort(_compare);\n'
            '    final undatedItems =\n'
            '        validItems\n'
            '            .where((item) => item.actionTiming == V2ActionTiming.undated)\n'
            '            .toList(growable: true)\n'
            '          ..sort(_compare);\n'
            '    final futureItems =\n'
            '        validItems\n'
            '            .where((item) => item.actionTiming == V2ActionTiming.future)\n'
            '            .toList(growable: true)\n'
            '          ..sort(_compare);\n'
            '    final recentStudents =\n'
            '        data.students\n'
            '            .where((student) => student.lastActivityAt != null)\n'
            '            .toList(growable: true)\n'
            '          ..sort(\n'
            '            (left, right) =>\n'
            '                right.lastActivityAt!.compareTo(left.lastActivityAt!),\n'
            '          );\n'
            '\n'
            '    return SingleChildScrollView(\n'
            '      padding: EdgeInsets.all(compact ? 18 : 32),\n'
            '      child: Center(\n'
            '        child: ConstrainedBox(\n'
            '          constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 900),\n'
            '          child: Column(\n'
            '            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
            '            children: [\n'
            '              Row(\n'
            '                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
            '                children: [\n'
            '                  Expanded(\n'
            '                    child: Column(\n'
            '                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
            '                      children: [\n'
            '                        Text(\n'
            '                          \'今日\',\n'
            '                          style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.headlineSmall,\n'
            '                        ),\n'
            '                        const SizedBox(height: 5),\n'
            '                        Text(\n'
            '                          _todayLabel(data.businessDate),\n'
            '                          style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall,\n'
            '                        ),\n'
            '                        const SizedBox(height: 4),\n'
            '                        Text(\n'
            '                          \'先处理已经安排好的跟进。\',\n'
            '                          style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall,\n'
            '                        ),\n'
            '                      ],\n'
            '                    ),\n'
            '                  ),\n'
            '                  const SizedBox(width: 12),\n'
            '                  FilledButton.tonalIcon(\n'
            '                    key: const Key(\'v2-today-quick-capture\'),\n'
            '                    onPressed: () => _showV2QuickCaptureStudentPicker(context),\n'
            '                    icon: const Icon(Icons.note_add_outlined, size: 18),\n'
            '                    label: const Text(\'记录问题\'),\n'
            '                  ),\n'
            '                  if (compact && onOpenMore != null) ...[\n'
            '                    const SizedBox(width: 4),\n'
            '                    IconButton(\n'
            '                      key: const Key(\'v2-compact-more\'),\n'
            '                      tooltip: \'更多操作\',\n'
            '                      onPressed: onOpenMore,\n'
            '                      icon: const Icon(Icons.more_vert),\n'
            '                    ),\n'
            '                  ],\n'
            '                ],\n'
            '              ),\n'
            '              const SizedBox(height: 28),\n'
            '              if (currentItems.isEmpty && undatedItems.isEmpty)\n'
            '                Text(\n'
            '                  \'今天暂时没有需要处理的提醒\',\n'
            '                  style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium,\n'
            '                ),\n'
            '              if (currentItems.isNotEmpty) ...[\n'
            '                _SectionTitle(title: \'现在要做\', count: currentItems.length),\n'
            '                const SizedBox(height: 8),\n'
            '                for (final item in currentItems)\n'
            '                  _TodayAction(item: item, onOpenCase: onOpenCase),\n'
            '              ],\n'
            '              if (currentItems.isNotEmpty && undatedItems.isNotEmpty)\n'
            '                const SizedBox(height: 28),\n'
            '              if (undatedItems.isNotEmpty) ...[\n'
            '                _SectionTitle(title: \'待安排\', count: undatedItems.length),\n'
            '                const SizedBox(height: 6),\n'
            '                Text(\n'
            '                  \'这些提醒已经明确，只差安排日期。\',\n'
            '                  style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall,\n'
            '                ),\n'
            '                const SizedBox(height: 6),\n'
            '                for (final item in undatedItems)\n'
            '                  _TodayAction(item: item, onOpenCase: onOpenCase),\n'
            '              ],\n'
            '              if (futureItems.isNotEmpty) ...[\n'
            '                const SizedBox(height: 28),\n'
            '                Divider(color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.outlineVariant),\n'
            '                ExpansionTile(\n'
            '                  key: const Key(\'v2-today-future-section\'),\n'
            '                  tilePadding: EdgeInsets.zero,\n'
            '                  childrenPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,\n'
            '                  leading: const Icon(Icons.event_outlined),\n'
            '                  title: const Text(\'之后\'),\n'
            '                  subtitle: Text(\'${futureItems.length} 项已安排的后续行动\'),\n'
            '                  children: [\n'
            '                    for (final item in futureItems)\n'
            '                      _TodayAction(item: item, onOpenCase: onOpenCase),\n'
            '                  ],\n'
            '                ),\n'
            '              ],\n'
            '              if (recentStudents.isNotEmpty) ...[\n'
            '                const SizedBox(height: 28),\n'
            '                Divider(color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.outlineVariant),\n'
            '                const SizedBox(height: 18),\n'
            '                const _SectionTitle(title: \'最近学生\'),\n'
            '                const SizedBox(height: 8),\n'
            '                for (final student in recentStudents.take(5))\n'
            '                  _TodayRecentStudentRow(\n'
            '                    student: student,\n'
            '                    businessDate: data.businessDate,\n'
            '                    onTap: () => onOpenStudent(student),\n'
            '                  ),\n'
            '              ],\n'
            '            ],\n'
            '          ),\n'
            '        ),\n'
            '      ),\n'
            '    );\n'
            '  }\n'
            '}\n'
            '\n'
            'String _todayLabel(DateTime? businessDate) {\n'
            '  const weekdays = [\'周一\', \'周二\', \'周三\', \'周四\', \'周五\', \'周六\', \'周日\'];\n'
            '  final now = businessDate ?? DateTime.now();\n'
            '  return \'${now.month} 月 ${now.day} 日 · ${weekdays[now.weekday - 1]}\';\n'
            '}\n'
            '\n'
            'class _TodayAction extends StatelessWidget {\n'
            '  const _TodayAction({required this.item, required this.onOpenCase});\n'
            '\n'
            '  final V2FocusItem item;\n'
            '  final ValueChanged<V2FocusItem> onOpenCase;\n'
            '\n'
            '  @override\n'
            '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
            '    final scheme = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;\n'
            '    final student = V2WorkspaceDataScope.of(context)\n'
            '        .studentForFocusItemOrNull(item);\n'
            '    if (student == null) return const SizedBox.shrink();\n'
            '    final controller = _V2RuntimeScope.maybeOf(context)?.workflowController;\n'
            '    final pendingAction = controller?.pendingActionFor(item.id);\n'
            '    final actionTitle = pendingAction?.title.trim().isNotEmpty == true\n'
            '        ? pendingAction!.title.trim()\n'
            '        : _displayNextStep(item.nextStep);\n'
            '    final status = _todayActionStatus(item);\n'
            '    return InkWell(\n'
            '      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),\n'
            '      onTap: () => onOpenCase(item),\n'
            '      child: Padding(\n'
            '        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 15),\n'
            '        child: Row(\n'
            '          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
            '          children: [\n'
            '            Container(\n'
            '              width: 2,\n'
            '              height: 62,\n'
            '              color: item.actionTiming == V2ActionTiming.overdue\n'
            '                  ? const Color(0xFFB77728)\n'
            '                  : scheme.primary,\n'
            '            ),\n'
            '            const SizedBox(width: 15),\n'
            '            Expanded(\n'
            '              child: Column(\n'
            '                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
            '                children: [\n'
            '                  Text(\n'
            '                    actionTitle,\n'
            '                    style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleMedium,\n'
            '                  ),\n'
            '                  const SizedBox(height: 4),\n'
            '                  Text(\n'
            '                    \'${student.name} · ${item.subject}\',\n'
            '                    style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall,\n'
            '                  ),\n'
            '                  const SizedBox(height: 3),\n'
            '                  Text(\n'
            '                    item.pendingVerification\n'
            '                        ? \'${item.title} · 继续关注\'\n'
            '                        : item.title,\n'
            '                    style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall,\n'
            '                  ),\n'
            '                  if (pendingAction != null) ...[\n'
            '                    const SizedBox(height: 7),\n'
            '                    Wrap(\n'
            '                      spacing: 4,\n'
            '                      runSpacing: 4,\n'
            '                      children: [\n'
            '                        if (pendingAction.canComplete)\n'
            '                          TextButton.icon(\n'
            '                            key: ValueKey<String>(\n'
            '                              \'v2-today-complete-${item.id}\',\n'
            '                            ),\n'
            '                            onPressed: () => _showV2CompleteCurrentAction(\n'
            '                              context,\n'
            '                              student,\n'
            '                              item,\n'
            '                            ),\n'
            '                            icon: const Icon(\n'
            '                              Icons.check_circle_outline,\n'
            '                              size: 17,\n'
            '                            ),\n'
            '                            label: const Text(\'处理\'),\n'
            '                          )\n'
            '                        else\n'
            '                          TextButton.icon(\n'
            '                            key: ValueKey<String>(\'v2-today-review-${item.id}\'),\n'
            '                            onPressed: () => onOpenCase(item),\n'
            '                            icon: const Icon(Icons.open_in_new, size: 17),\n'
            '                            label: const Text(\'查看问题\'),\n'
            '                          ),\n'
            '                        TextButton.icon(\n'
            '                          key: ValueKey<String>(\n'
            '                            \'v2-today-reschedule-${item.id}\',\n'
            '                          ),\n'
            '                          onPressed: () => _showV2RescheduleCurrentAction(\n'
            '                            context,\n'
            '                            student,\n'
            '                            item,\n'
            '                          ),\n'
            '                          icon: const Icon(\n'
            '                            Icons.event_repeat_outlined,\n'
            '                            size: 17,\n'
            '                          ),\n'
            '                          label: Text(\n'
            '                            pendingAction.dueOn == null ? \'安排日期\' : \'改期\',\n'
            '                          ),\n'
            '                        ),\n'
            '                      ],\n'
            '                    ),\n'
            '                  ],\n'
            '                ],\n'
            '              ),\n'
            '            ),\n'
            '            if (status.isNotEmpty) ...[\n'
            '              const SizedBox(width: 10),\n'
            '              Text(status, style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall),\n'
            '            ],\n'
            '            const SizedBox(width: 4),\n'
            '            const Icon(Icons.chevron_right, size: 20),\n'
            '          ],\n'
            '        ),\n'
            '      ),\n'
            '    );\n'
            '  }\n'
            '}\n'
            '\n'
            'String _todayActionStatus(V2FocusItem item) => switch (item.actionTiming) {\n'
            '  V2ActionTiming.overdue =>\n'
            '    item.dueLabel == \'待安排\' ? \'已逾期\' : \'已逾期 · ${item.dueLabel}\',\n'
            '  V2ActionTiming.today => \'今天\',\n'
            '  V2ActionTiming.undated => \'待安排日期\',\n'
            '  V2ActionTiming.future => item.dueLabel,\n'
            '  null => \'\',\n'
            '};\n'
            '\n'
            'class _TodayRecentStudentRow extends StatelessWidget {\n'
            '  const _TodayRecentStudentRow({\n'
            '    required this.student,\n'
            '    required this.businessDate,\n'
            '    required this.onTap,\n'
            '  });\n'
            '\n'
            '  final V2Student student;\n'
            '  final DateTime? businessDate;\n'
            '  final VoidCallback onTap;\n'
            '\n'
            '  @override\n'
            '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
            '    return InkWell(\n'
            '      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),\n'
            '      onTap: onTap,\n'
            '      child: Padding(\n'
            '        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 11),\n'
            '        child: Row(\n'
            '          children: [\n'
            '            Expanded(\n'
            '              child: Column(\n'
            '                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
            '                children: [\n'
            '                  Text(\n'
            '                    student.name,\n'
            '                    style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleSmall,\n'
            '                  ),\n'
            '                  const SizedBox(height: 3),\n'
            '                  Text(\n'
            '                    \'${student.grade} · ${student.subjects.join(\' / \')}\',\n'
            '                    style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall,\n'
            '                  ),\n'
            '                ],\n'
            '              ),\n'
            '            ),\n'
            '            Text(\n'
            '              _recentActivityLabel(student.lastActivityAt!, businessDate),\n'
            '              style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall,\n'
            '            ),\n'
            '            const SizedBox(width: 4),\n'
            '            const Icon(Icons.chevron_right, size: 20),\n'
            '          ],\n'
            '        ),\n'
            '      ),\n'
            '    );\n'
            '  }\n'
            '}\n'
            '\n'
            'String _recentActivityLabel(DateTime value, DateTime? businessDate) {\n'
            '  final reference = businessDate ?? DateTime.now();\n'
            '  final referenceDay = DateTime(reference.year, reference.month, reference.day);\n'
            '  final valueDay = DateTime(value.year, value.month, value.day);\n'
            '  final days = referenceDay.difference(valueDay).inDays;\n'
            '  if (days <= 0) return \'今天有记录\';\n'
            '  if (days == 1) return \'昨天有记录\';\n'
            '  if (days < 7) return \'$days 天前\';\n'
            '  return \'${value.month} 月 ${value.day} 日\';\n'
            '}\n'
            '\n'
            'class _CaseIndexPane extends StatefulWidget {\n'
            '  const _CaseIndexPane({\n'
            '    required this.onOpenCase,\n'
            '    this.compact = false,\n'
            '    this.onOpenMore,\n'
            '  });\n'
            '\n'
            '  final ValueChanged<V2FocusItem> onOpenCase;\n'
            '  final bool compact;\n'
            '  final VoidCallback? onOpenMore;\n'
            '\n'
            '  @override\n'
            '  State<_CaseIndexPane> createState() => _CaseIndexPaneState();\n'
            '}\n'
            '\n'
            'class _CaseIndexPaneState extends State<_CaseIndexPane> {\n'
            '  final _searchController = TextEditingController();\n'
            '  String _query = \'\';\n'
            '  bool _showClosed = false;\n'
            '\n'
            '  @override\n'
            '  void dispose() {\n'
            '    _searchController.dispose();\n'
            '    super.dispose();\n'
            '  }\n'
            '\n'
            '  List<V2FocusItem> _visibleItems(V2WorkspaceData data) {\n'
            '    final query = _query.trim().toLowerCase();\n'
            '    final sourceItems = _showClosed ? data.closedItems : data.focusItems;\n'
            '    final validItems = sourceItems\n'
            '        .where((item) => data.studentForFocusItemOrNull(item) != null)\n'
            '        .toList(growable: false);\n'
            '    if (query.isEmpty) {\n'
            '      return validItems;\n'
            '    }\n'
            '    return validItems\n'
            '        .where((item) {\n'
            '          final student = data.studentForFocusItem(item);\n'
            '          final haystack = [\n'
            '            student.name,\n'
            '            student.grade,\n'
            '            item.subject,\n'
            '            item.title,\n'
            '            item.summary,\n'
            '            item.nextStep,\n'
            '            student.teacherSummary,\n'
            '          ].join(\' \').toLowerCase();\n'

            'Future<void> _showV2ReopenClosedCase(\n'
            '  BuildContext context,\n'
            '  V2Student student,\n'
            '  V2FocusItem item,\n'
            ') async {\n'
            '  final runtime = _V2RuntimeScope.maybeOf(context);\n'

            '    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)\n'
            '        .showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(message)));\n'
            '    return;\n'
            '  }\n'
            '  if (!context.mounted) return;\n'
            '\n'
            '  final saved = await showV2ReopenCaseComposer(\n'
            '    context,\n'
            '    studentName: student.name,\n'
            '    subject: item.subject,\n'
            '    caseTitle: item.title,\n'
            '    businessDate: controller.businessDate,\n'
            '    pendingDraft: pendingDraft,\n'

            '        recurrenceSummary: draft.recurrenceSummary,\n'
            '        nextActionTitle: draft.nextActionTitle,\n'
            '        nextActionDueOn: draft.nextActionDueOn,\n'
            '      ),\n'
            '    ),\n'
            '  );\n'
            '  if (saved && context.mounted) {\n'
            '    runtime?.onWorkspaceChanged?.call();\n'
            '  }\n'
            '}\n'

            '            ),\n'

            '                      setDialogState(() {\n'

            '                        errorText = null;\n'

            '                        await controller.voidCase(\n'
            '                          operationId: operationId,\n'
            '                          caseId: item.id,\n'
            '                        );\n'
            '                        if (dialogContext.mounted) {\n'
            '                          Navigator.of(dialogContext).pop(true);\n'
            '                        }\n'

            '                    )\n'
            '                  : const Text(\'删除问题\'),\n'
            '            ),\n'
            '          ],\n'
            '        ),\n'
            '      );\n'
            '    },\n'
            '  );\n'
            '\n'
            '  if (removed == true && context.mounted) {\n'
            '    runtime?.onWorkspaceChanged?.call();\n'
            '    return true;\n'
            '  }\n'
            '  return false;\n'
            '}\n'

            '}) async {\n'
            '  final runtime = _V2RuntimeScope.maybeOf(context);\n'
            '  final controller = runtime?.workflowController;\n'
            '  final storedOperationId = initialDraft?.state[\'operation_id\'];\n'
            '  final storedPhotoOperationId =\n'
            '      initialDraft?.state[\'photo_evidence_operation_id\'];\n'
            '  final operationId =\n'
            '      storedOperationId is String && storedOperationId.trim().isNotEmpty\n'
            '      ? storedOperationId\n'
            '      : controller == null\n'
            '      ? null\n'
            '      : createOperationId();\n'
            '  final photoEvidenceOperationId =\n'
            '      storedPhotoOperationId is String &&\n'
            '          storedPhotoOperationId.trim().isNotEmpty\n'
            '      ? storedPhotoOperationId\n'
            '      : controller == null\n'
            '      ? null\n'
            '      : createOperationId();\n'
            '  final store = runtime?.composerDraftStore;\n'
            '  final scopeKey = runtime?.composerDraftScopeKey;\n'
            '  final persistence =\n'
            '      store != null &&\n'
            '          scopeKey != null &&\n'
            '          operationId != null &&\n'
            '          photoEvidenceOperationId != null\n'
            '      ? V2ProgressPersistence(\n'
            '          store: store,\n'
            '          scopeKey: scopeKey,\n'
            '          studentId: student.id,\n'
            '          subject: item.subject,\n'
            '          caseId: item.id,\n'
            '          operationId: operationId,\n'
            '          photoEvidenceOperationId: photoEvidenceOperationId,\n'
            '          initialDraft: initialDraft,\n'
            '        )\n'
            '      : null;\n'
            '  final saved = await showV2ProgressComposer(\n'
            '    context,\n'
            '    studentName: student.name,\n'
            '    subject: item.subject,\n'
            '    caseTitle: item.title,\n'
            '    canCompleteCurrentAction:\n'
            '        controller?.hasPendingPrimaryAction(item.id) ?? false,\n'
            '    businessDate: controller?.businessDate,\n'
            '    persistence: persistence,\n'
            '    onSave: controller == null\n'
            '        ? null\n'
            '        : (draft) async {\n'
            '            await controller.recordProgress(\n'
            '              V2ProgressWrite(\n'
            '                operationId: operationId!,\n'
            '                photoEvidenceOperationId: photoEvidenceOperationId!,\n'
            '                caseId: item.id,\n'
            '                progressKind: _domainProgressKind(draft.kind),\n'
            '                summary: draft.body,\n'
            '                assessmentResult: draft.kind == V2ProgressKind.assessment\n'
            '                    ? _domainAssessmentResult(draft.assessmentResult)\n'
            '                    : null,\n'
            '                completeCurrentAction: draft.completeCurrentAction,\n'
            '                nextStep: _domainNextStep(draft.nextStep),\n'
            '                nextActionTitle: draft.nextStep == V2NextStep.remind\n'
            '                    ? draft.reminderTitle\n'
            '                    : null,\n'
            '                nextActionDueOn: draft.nextStep == V2NextStep.remind\n'
            '                    ? draft.reminderDate\n'
            '                    : null,\n'
            '                closeReason: draft.nextStep == V2NextStep.close\n'
            '                    ? _domainCloseReason(draft.closeReason)\n'
            '                    : null,\n'
            '                attachments: draft.attachments,\n'
            '              ),\n'
            '            );\n'
            '          },\n'
            '  );\n'
            '  if (saved && context.mounted) {\n'
            '    runtime?.onWorkspaceChanged?.call();\n'
            '  }\n'
            '}\n'
            '\n'
            'CaseProgressKind _domainProgressKind(V2ProgressKind kind) => switch (kind) {\n'
            '  V2ProgressKind.observation => CaseProgressKind.observation,\n'
            '  V2ProgressKind.intervention => CaseProgressKind.intervention,\n'
            '  V2ProgressKind.assessment => CaseProgressKind.assessment,\n'
            '};\n'
            '\n'
            'CaseAssessmentResult? _domainAssessmentResult(V2AssessmentResult? result) =>\n'
            '    switch (result) {\n'
            '      null => null,\n'
            '      V2AssessmentResult.passed => CaseAssessmentResult.passed,\n'
            '      V2AssessmentResult.partial => CaseAssessmentResult.partial,\n'
            '      V2AssessmentResult.notPassed => CaseAssessmentResult.notPassed,\n'
            '    };\n'
            '\n'
            'CaseProgressNextStep _domainNextStep(V2NextStep step) => switch (step) {\n'
            '  V2NextStep.continueTracking => CaseProgressNextStep.continueTracking,\n'
            '  V2NextStep.remind => CaseProgressNextStep.remind,\n'
            '  V2NextStep.close => CaseProgressNextStep.close,\n'
            '};\n'
            '\n'
            'CaseClosureReason _domainCloseReason(V2CloseReason reason) => switch (reason) {\n'
            '  V2CloseReason.resolved => CaseClosureReason.resolved,\n'
            '  V2CloseReason.pauseTracking => CaseClosureReason.pauseTracking,\n'
            '  V2CloseReason.notIssue => CaseClosureReason.notIssue,\n'
            '  V2CloseReason.other => CaseClosureReason.other,\n'
            '};\n'
            '\n'
            'Future<void> _showV2ProgressCasePicker(\n'
            '  BuildContext context,\n'
            '  V2Student student,\n'
            ') async {\n'
            '  final items = V2WorkspaceDataScope.of(context).focusItemsForStudent(student);\n'
            '  if (items.isEmpty) {\n'
            '    return;\n'
            '  }\n'
            '\n'
            '  Widget choices(BuildContext sheetContext) => ListView.separated(\n'
            '    shrinkWrap: true,\n'
            '    itemCount: items.length,\n'
            '    separatorBuilder: (_, _) => Divider(\n'
            '      height: 1,\n'
            '      color: Theme.of(sheetContext).colorScheme.outlineVariant,\n'
            '    ),\n'
            '    itemBuilder: (_, index) {\n'
            '      final item = items[index];\n'
            '      return ListTile(\n'
            '        title: Text(item.title),\n'
            '        subtitle: Text(item.subject),\n'
            '        trailing: const Icon(Icons.chevron_right),\n'
            '        onTap: () => Navigator.of(sheetContext).pop(item),\n'
            '      );\n'
            '    },\n'
            '  );\n'
            '\n'
            '  final compact = MediaQuery.sizeOf(context).width < 720;\n'
            '  final selected = compact\n'
            '      ? await showModalBottomSheet<V2FocusItem>(\n'
            '          context: context,\n'
            '          useSafeArea: true,\n'
            '          builder: (sheetContext) => Padding(\n'
            '            padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(18, 18, 18, 28),\n'
            '            child: Column(\n'
            '              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,\n'
            '              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
            '              children: [\n'
            '                Text(\n'
            '                  \'选择要记录进展的问题\',\n'
            '                  style: Theme.of(sheetContext).textTheme.titleLarge,\n'
            '                ),\n'
            '                const SizedBox(height: 12),\n'
            '                choices(sheetContext),\n'
            '              ],\n'
            '            ),\n'
            '          ),\n'
            '        )\n'
