            '      : await showDialog<V2FocusItem>(\n'
            '          context: context,\n'
            '          builder: (dialogContext) => AlertDialog(\n'
            '            title: const Text(\'选择要记录进展的问题\'),\n'
            '            content: SizedBox(width: 420, child: choices(dialogContext)),\n'
            '          ),\n'
            '        );\n'
            '  if (selected == null || !context.mounted) {\n'
            '    return;\n'
            '  }\n'
            '  await _showV2ProgressForCase(context, student, selected);\n'
            '}\n'
            '\n'
            'class V2WorkspacePreview extends StatefulWidget {\n'
            '  const V2WorkspacePreview({\n'
            '    super.key,\n'
            '    this.data = v2FixtureWorkspaceData,\n'
            '    this.workflowController,\n'
            '    this.composerDraftStore,\n'
            '    this.composerDraftScopeKey,\n'
            '    this.evidenceAttachmentRepository,\n'
            '    this.onExportStudent,\n'
            '    this.onExportMyStudents,\n'
            '    this.managementPageBuilder,\n'
            '    this.updateService,\n'
            '    this.updateInstaller,\n'
            '    this.appVersion,\n'
            '    this.onSignOut,\n'
            '    this.onRefresh,\n'
            '    this.onWorkspaceChanged,\n'
            '  });\n'
            '\n'
            '  final V2WorkspaceData data;\n'
            '  final V2WorkflowController? workflowController;\n'
            '  final ComposerDraftStore? composerDraftStore;\n'
            '  final String? composerDraftScopeKey;\n'
            '  final EvidenceAttachmentRepository? evidenceAttachmentRepository;\n'
            '  final V2StudentExport? onExportStudent;\n'
            '  final V2WorkspaceExport? onExportMyStudents;\n'
            '  final WidgetBuilder? managementPageBuilder;\n'
            '  final UpdateService? updateService;\n'
            '  final UpdateInstaller? updateInstaller;\n'
            '  final String? appVersion;\n'
            '  final VoidCallback? onSignOut;\n'
            '  final V2WorkspaceRefresh? onRefresh;\n'
            '  final VoidCallback? onWorkspaceChanged;\n'
            '\n'
            '  @override\n'
            '  State<V2WorkspacePreview> createState() => _V2WorkspacePreviewState();\n'
            '}\n'
            '\n'
            'class _V2WorkspacePreviewState extends State<V2WorkspacePreview> {\n'
            '  int _destination = 1;\n'
            '  V2Student? _selectedStudent;\n'
            '  V2FocusItem? _selectedCase;\n'
            '  bool _showCase = false;\n'
            '  bool _checkingForUpdates = false;\n'
            '  bool _refreshing = false;\n'
            '  bool _composerDraftRecoveryScheduled = false;\n'
            '\n'
            '  @override\n'
            '  void initState() {\n'
            '    super.initState();\n'
            '    _reconcileSelection();\n'
            '  }\n'
            '\n'
            '  @override\n'
            '  void didUpdateWidget(covariant V2WorkspacePreview oldWidget) {\n'
            '    super.didUpdateWidget(oldWidget);\n'
            '    if (!identical(oldWidget.data, widget.data)) {\n'
            '      _reconcileSelection();\n'
            '    }\n'
            '    if (oldWidget.composerDraftScopeKey != widget.composerDraftScopeKey ||\n'
            '        oldWidget.composerDraftStore != widget.composerDraftStore) {\n'
            '      _composerDraftRecoveryScheduled = false;\n'
            '    }\n'
            '  }\n'
            '\n'
            '  void _scheduleComposerDraftRecovery(BuildContext scopedContext) {\n'
            '    if (_composerDraftRecoveryScheduled ||\n'
            '        widget.composerDraftStore == null ||\n'
            '        widget.composerDraftScopeKey == null) {\n'
            '      return;\n'
            '    }\n'
            '    _composerDraftRecoveryScheduled = true;\n'
            '    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {\n'
            '      if (mounted && scopedContext.mounted) {\n'
            '        unawaited(_restoreComposerDraft(scopedContext));\n'
            '      }\n'
            '    });\n'
            '  }\n'
            '\n'
            '  Future<void> _clearComposerDraft() async {\n'
            '    final store = widget.composerDraftStore;\n'
            '    final scopeKey = widget.composerDraftScopeKey;\n'
            '    if (store == null || scopeKey == null) return;\n'
            '    try {\n'
            '      await store.clear(scopeKey);\n'
            '    } catch (_) {}\n'
            '  }\n'
            '\n'
            '  Future<void> _restoreComposerDraft(BuildContext scopedContext) async {\n'
            '    final store = widget.composerDraftStore;\n'
            '    final scopeKey = widget.composerDraftScopeKey;\n'
            '    if (store == null || scopeKey == null) return;\n'
            '\n'
            '    ComposerDraftSnapshot? draft;\n'
            '    try {\n'
            '      draft = await store.load(scopeKey);\n'
            '    } catch (_) {\n'
            '      await _clearComposerDraft();\n'
            '      return;\n'
            '    }\n'
            '    if (!mounted || !scopedContext.mounted || draft == null) return;\n'
            '\n'
            '    V2Student? student;\n'
            '    for (final candidate in widget.data.students) {\n'
            '      if (candidate.id == draft.studentId) {\n'
            '        student = candidate;\n'
            '        break;\n'
            '      }\n'
            '    }\n'
            '    if (student == null) {\n'
            '      await _clearComposerDraft();\n'
            '      return;\n'
            '    }\n'
            '\n'
            '    final operationId = draft.state[\'operation_id\'];\n'
            '    if (operationId is! String || operationId.trim().isEmpty) {\n'
            '      await _clearComposerDraft();\n'
            '      return;\n'
            '    }\n'
            '\n'
            '    if (draft.kind == \'quick_capture\') {\n'
            '      final subject = draft.subject;\n'
            '      if (subject != null && !student.subjects.contains(subject)) {\n'
            '        await _clearComposerDraft();\n'
            '        return;\n'
            '      }\n'
            '      if (!mounted || !scopedContext.mounted) return;\n'
            '      await _showV2QuickCaptureForStudent(\n'
            '        scopedContext,\n'
            '        student,\n'
            '        initialDraft: draft,\n'
            '      );\n'
            '      return;\n'
            '    }\n'
            '\n'
            '    if (draft.kind == \'progress\') {\n'
            '      final subject = draft.subject;\n'
            '      final caseId = draft.caseId;\n'
            '      final photoOperationId = draft.state[\'photo_evidence_operation_id\'];\n'
            '      if (subject == null ||\n'
            '          !student.subjects.contains(subject) ||\n'
            '          caseId == null ||\n'
            '          photoOperationId is! String ||\n'
            '          photoOperationId.trim().isEmpty) {\n'
            '        await _clearComposerDraft();\n'
            '        return;\n'
            '      }\n'
            '      V2FocusItem? item;\n'
            '      for (final candidate in widget.data.focusItems) {\n'
            '        if (candidate.id == caseId &&\n'
            '            candidate.studentId == student.id &&\n'
            '            candidate.subject == subject &&\n'
            '            !candidate.closed) {\n'
            '          item = candidate;\n'
            '          break;\n'
            '        }\n'
            '      }\n'
            '      if (item == null) {\n'
            '        await _clearComposerDraft();\n'
            '        return;\n'
            '      }\n'
            '      _openCase(item);\n'
            '      await Future<void>.delayed(Duration.zero);\n'
            '      if (!mounted || !scopedContext.mounted) return;\n'
            '      await _showV2ProgressForCase(\n'
            '        scopedContext,\n'
            '        student,\n'
            '        item,\n'
            '        initialDraft: draft,\n'
            '      );\n'
            '      return;\n'
            '    }\n'
            '\n'
            '    await _clearComposerDraft();\n'
            '  }\n'
            '\n'
            '  void _reconcileSelection() {\n'
            '    final students = widget.data.students;\n'
            '    if (students.isEmpty) {\n'
            '      _selectedStudent = null;\n'
            '      _selectedCase = null;\n'
            '      _showCase = false;\n'
            '      return;\n'
            '    }\n'
            '\n'
            '    final currentStudentId = _selectedStudent?.id;\n'
            '    _selectedStudent = students.firstWhere(\n'
            '      (student) => student.id == currentStudentId,\n'
            '      orElse: () => students.first,\n'
            '    );\n'
            '\n'
            '    final currentCaseId = _selectedCase?.id;\n'
            '    if (currentCaseId == null) {\n'
            '      return;\n'
            '    }\n'
            '    final matchingCases = widget.data.focusItems\n'
            '        .where((item) => item.id == currentCaseId)\n'
            '        .toList(growable: false);\n'
            '    if (matchingCases.isEmpty ||\n'
            '        matchingCases.first.studentId != _selectedStudent!.id) {\n'
            '      _selectedCase = null;\n'
            '      _showCase = false;\n'
            '    } else {\n'
            '      _selectedCase = matchingCases.first;\n'
            '    }\n'
            '  }\n'
            '\n'
            '  void _openStudent(V2Student student) {\n'
            '    setState(() {\n'
            '      _selectedStudent = student;\n'
            '      _selectedCase = null;\n'
            '      _showCase = false;\n'
            '    });\n'
            '  }\n'
            '\n'
            '  void _openCase(V2FocusItem item) {\n'
            '    final student = widget.data.studentForFocusItemOrNull(item);\n'
            '    if (student == null) {\n'
            '      return;\n'
            '    }\n'
            '    setState(() {\n'
            '      _destination = 1;\n'
            '      _selectedStudent = student;\n'
            '      _selectedCase = item;\n'
            '      _showCase = true;\n'
            '    });\n'
            '  }\n'
            '\n'
            '  void _closeCase() => setState(() => _showCase = false);\n'
            '\n'
            '  void _changeDestination(int value) {\n'
            '    setState(() {\n'
            '      _destination = value;\n'
            '      _showCase = false;\n'
            '    });\n'
            '  }\n'
            '\n'
            '  Future<void> _openManagement(BuildContext context) async {\n'
            '    final builder = widget.managementPageBuilder;\n'
            '    if (builder == null) return;\n'
            '    await Navigator.of(context)\n'
            '        .push<void>(MaterialPageRoute<void>(builder: builder));\n'
            '  }\n'
            '\n'
            '  Future<void> _checkForUpdates(BuildContext context) async {\n'
            '    final service = widget.updateService;\n'
            '    final installer = widget.updateInstaller;\n'
            '    if (service == null || installer == null || _checkingForUpdates) return;\n'
            '    setState(() => _checkingForUpdates = true);\n'
            '    try {\n'
            '      await runV2UpdateFlow(context, service: service, installer: installer);\n'
            '    } finally {\n'
            '      if (mounted) setState(() => _checkingForUpdates = false);\n'
            '    }\n'
            '  }\n'
            '\n'
            '  Future<void> _refreshWorkspace() async {\n'
            '    final refresh = widget.onRefresh;\n'
            '    if (refresh == null || _refreshing) return;\n'
            '    setState(() => _refreshing = true);\n'
            '    try {\n'
            '      await refresh();\n'
            '    } finally {\n'
            '      if (mounted) setState(() => _refreshing = false);\n'
            '    }\n'
            '  }\n'
            '\n'
            '  void _afterMenuClose(BuildContext menuContext, VoidCallback action) {\n'
            '    Navigator.of(menuContext).pop();\n'
            '    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {\n'
            '      if (mounted) action();\n'
            '    });\n'
            '  }\n'
            '\n'
            '  Future<void> _showOperationGuide(BuildContext context) async {\n'
            '    if (MediaQuery.sizeOf(context).width < 720) {\n'
            '      await showModalBottomSheet<void>(\n'
            '        context: context,\n'
            '        useSafeArea: true,\n'
            '        showDragHandle: true,\n'
            '        isScrollControlled: true,\n'
            '        builder: (sheetContext) => const FractionallySizedBox(\n'
            '          heightFactor: 0.82,\n'
            '          child: Padding(\n'
            '            padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 4, 20, 24),\n'
            '            child: _V2OperationGuide(showTitle: true),\n'
            '          ),\n'
            '        ),\n'
            '      );\n'
            '      return;\n'
            '    }\n'
            '\n'
            '    await showDialog<void>(\n'
            '      context: context,\n'
            '      builder: (dialogContext) => const AlertDialog(\n'
            '        title: Text(\'操作指南\'),\n'
            '        content: SizedBox(width: 520, height: 500, child: _V2OperationGuide()),\n'
            '      ),\n'
            '    );\n'
            '  }\n'
            '\n'
            '  Future<void> _showWorkspaceMenu(BuildContext context) async {\n'
            '    Widget menu(BuildContext menuContext) => SafeArea(\n'
            '      child: Padding(\n'
            '        padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(12, 12, 12, 18),\n'
            '        child: Column(\n'
            '          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,\n'
            '          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,\n'
            '          children: [\n'
            '            if (widget.onRefresh != null)\n'
            '              ListTile(\n'
            '                key: const Key(\'v2-menu-refresh\'),\n'
            '                leading: _refreshing\n'
            '                    ? const SizedBox(\n'
            '                        width: 22,\n'
            '                        height: 22,\n'
            '                        child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2),\n'
            '                      )\n'
            '                    : const Icon(Icons.refresh_outlined),\n'
            '                title: Text(_refreshing ? \'正在刷新\' : \'刷新学情\'),\n'
            '                subtitle: const Text(\'重新读取最新学生与学情记录\'),\n'
            '                onTap: _refreshing\n'
            '                    ? null\n'
            '                    : () => _afterMenuClose(\n'
            '                        menuContext,\n'
            '                        () => _refreshWorkspace(),\n'
            '                      ),\n'
            '              ),\n'
            '            ListTile(\n'
            '              key: const Key(\'v2-menu-operation-guide\'),\n'
            '              leading: const Icon(Icons.help_outline),\n'
            '              title: const Text(\'操作指南\'),\n'
            '              subtitle: const Text(\'常用操作一页看懂\'),\n'
            '              onTap: () => _afterMenuClose(\n'
            '                menuContext,\n'
            '                () => _showOperationGuide(context),\n'
            '              ),\n'
            '            ),\n'
            '            if (widget.onExportMyStudents != null)\n'
            '              ListTile(\n'
            '                key: const Key(\'v2-menu-export-my-students\'),\n'
            '                leading: const Icon(Icons.download_outlined),\n'
            '                title: const Text(\'导出我的学生学情\'),\n'
            '                subtitle: const Text(\'导出当前有权限的学生、记录与图片\'),\n'
            '                onTap: () => _afterMenuClose(\n'
            '                  menuContext,\n'
            '                  () => unawaited(widget.onExportMyStudents!(context)),\n'
            '                ),\n'
            '              ),\n'
            '            if (widget.managementPageBuilder != null)\n'
            '              ListTile(\n'
            '                leading: const Icon(Icons.admin_panel_settings_outlined),\n'
            '                title: const Text(\'机构管理\'),\n'
            '                subtitle: const Text(\'成员、学生、学科与记录导出\'),\n'
            '                onTap: () => _afterMenuClose(\n'
            '                  menuContext,\n'
            '                  () => _openManagement(context),\n'
            '                ),\n'
            '              ),\n'
            '            ListTile(\n'
            '              leading: _checkingForUpdates\n'
            '                  ? const SizedBox(\n'
            '                      width: 24,\n'
            '                      height: 24,\n'
            '                      child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2),\n'
            '                    )\n'
            '                  : const Icon(Icons.system_update_alt_outlined),\n'
            '              title: const Text(\'检查更新\'),\n'
            '              subtitle: widget.appVersion == null\n'
            '                  ? null\n'
            '                  : Text(\'当前版本 ${widget.appVersion}\'),\n'
            '              onTap:\n'
            '                  _checkingForUpdates ||\n'
            '                      widget.updateService == null ||\n'
            '                      widget.updateInstaller == null\n'
            '                  ? null\n'
            '                  : () => _afterMenuClose(\n'
            '                      menuContext,\n'
            '                      () => _checkForUpdates(context),\n'
            '                    ),\n'
            '            ),\n'
            '            if (widget.onSignOut != null)\n'
            '              ListTile(\n'
            '                leading: const Icon(Icons.logout_outlined),\n'
            '                title: const Text(\'退出登录\'),\n'
            '                onTap: () => _afterMenuClose(menuContext, widget.onSignOut!),\n'
            '              ),\n'
            '          ],\n'
            '        ),\n'
            '      ),\n'
            '    );\n'
            '\n'
            '    if (MediaQuery.sizeOf(context).width < 720) {\n'
            '      await showModalBottomSheet<void>(\n'
            '        context: context,\n'
            '        useSafeArea: true,\n'
            '        showDragHandle: true,\n'
            '        builder: menu,\n'
            '      );\n'
            '    } else {\n'
            '      await showDialog<void>(\n'
            '        context: context,\n'
            '        builder: (dialogContext) => AlertDialog(\n'
            '          title: const Text(\'更多\'),\n'
            '          content: SizedBox(width: 390, child: menu(dialogContext)),\n'
            '        ),\n'
            '      );\n'
            '    }\n'
            '  }\n'
            '\n'
            '  @override\n'
            '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
            '    return _V2RuntimeScope(\n'
            '      workflowController: widget.workflowController,\n'
            '      evidenceAttachmentRepository: widget.evidenceAttachmentRepository,\n'
            '      composerDraftStore: widget.composerDraftStore,\n'
            '      composerDraftScopeKey: widget.composerDraftScopeKey,\n'
            '      studentExport: widget.onExportStudent,\n'
            '      onWorkspaceChanged: widget.onWorkspaceChanged,\n'
            '      child: V2WorkspaceDataScope(\n'
            '        data: widget.data,\n'
            '        child: Builder(\n'
            '          builder: (context) {\n'
            '            _scheduleComposerDraftRecovery(context);\n'
            '            if (widget.data.students.isEmpty) {\n'
            '              return _EmptyWorkspacePreview(\n'
            '                onOpenManagement: widget.managementPageBuilder == null\n'
            '                    ? null\n'
            '                    : () => _openManagement(context),\n'
            '                onOpenMore: () => _showWorkspaceMenu(context),\n'
            '              );\n'
            '            }\n'
            '            final selectedStudent =\n'
            '                _selectedStudent ?? widget.data.students.first;\n'
            '            return LayoutBuilder(\n'
            '              builder: (context, constraints) {\n'
            '                if (constraints.maxWidth < 720) {\n'
            '                  return _CompactWorkspace(\n'
            '                    destination: _destination,\n'
            '                    selectedStudent: selectedStudent,\n'
            '                    selectedCase: _selectedCase,\n'
            '                    showCase: _showCase,\n'
            '                    onDestinationChanged: _changeDestination,\n'
            '                    onStudentSelected: _openStudent,\n'
            '                    onOpenCase: _openCase,\n'
            '                    onBackFromCase: _closeCase,\n'
            '                    onOpenMore: () => _showWorkspaceMenu(context),\n'
            '                  );\n'
            '                }\n'
            '                return _DesktopWorkspace(\n'
            '                  destination: _destination,\n'
            '                  selectedStudent: selectedStudent,\n'
            '                  selectedCase: _selectedCase,\n'
            '                  showCase: _showCase,\n'
            '                  onDestinationChanged: _changeDestination,\n'
            '                  onStudentSelected: _openStudent,\n'
            '                  onOpenCase: _openCase,\n'
            '                  onBackFromCase: _closeCase,\n'
            '                  onRefresh: widget.onRefresh == null\n'
            '                      ? null\n'
            '                      : () => _refreshWorkspace(),\n'
            '                  refreshing: _refreshing,\n'
            '                  onManage: widget.managementPageBuilder == null\n'
            '                      ? null\n'
            '                      : () => _openManagement(context),\n'
            '                  onSettings: () => _showWorkspaceMenu(context),\n'
            '                );\n'
            '              },\n'
            '            );\n'
            '          },\n'
            '        ),\n'
            '      ),\n'
            '    );\n'
            '  }\n'
            '}\n'
            '\n'
            'class _QuietPaneTransition extends StatelessWidget {\n'
            '  const _QuietPaneTransition({\n'
            '    required this.transitionKey,\n'
            '    required this.child,\n'
            '  });\n'
            '\n'
            '  final Object transitionKey;\n'
            '  final Widget child;\n'
            '\n'
            '  @override\n'
            '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
            '    final enterDuration = AppMotion.effectiveDuration(\n'
            '      context,\n'
            '      AppMotion.standard,\n'
            '    );\n'
            '    final exitDuration = AppMotion.effectiveDuration(context, AppMotion.quick);\n'
            '    return AnimatedSwitcher(\n'
            '      duration: enterDuration,\n'
            '      reverseDuration: exitDuration,\n'
            '      switchInCurve: AppMotion.enter,\n'
            '      switchOutCurve: AppMotion.exit,\n'
            '      transitionBuilder: (child, animation) {\n'
            '        final curved = CurvedAnimation(\n'
            '          parent: animation,\n'
            '          curve: AppMotion.enter,\n'
            '        );\n'
            '        return FadeTransition(\n'
            '          opacity: curved,\n'
            '          child: SlideTransition(\n'
            '            position: Tween<Offset>(\n'
            '              begin: const Offset(0, 0.012),\n'
            '              end: Offset.zero,\n'
            '            ).animate(curved),\n'
            '            child: child,\n'
            '          ),\n'
            '        );\n'
            '      },\n'
            '      child: KeyedSubtree(key: ValueKey<Object>(transitionKey), child: child),\n'
            '    );\n'
            '  }\n'
            '}\n'
            '\n'
            'class _DesktopWorkspace extends StatelessWidget {\n'
            '  const _DesktopWorkspace({\n'
            '    required this.destination,\n'
            '    required this.selectedStudent,\n'
            '    required this.selectedCase,\n'
            '    required this.showCase,\n'
            '    required this.onDestinationChanged,\n'
            '    required this.onStudentSelected,\n'
            '    required this.onOpenCase,\n'
            '    required this.onBackFromCase,\n'
            '    required this.onSettings,\n'
            '    required this.refreshing,\n'
            '    this.onRefresh,\n'
            '    this.onManage,\n'
            '  });\n'
            '\n'
            '  final int destination;\n'
            '  final V2Student selectedStudent;\n'
            '  final V2FocusItem? selectedCase;\n'
            '  final bool showCase;\n'
            '  final ValueChanged<int> onDestinationChanged;\n'
            '  final ValueChanged<V2Student> onStudentSelected;\n'
            '  final ValueChanged<V2FocusItem> onOpenCase;\n'
            '  final VoidCallback onBackFromCase;\n'
            '  final VoidCallback? onRefresh;\n'
            '  final bool refreshing;\n'
            '  final VoidCallback? onManage;\n'
            '  final VoidCallback onSettings;\n'
            '\n'
            '  @override\n'
            '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
            '    final border = Theme.of(context).colorScheme.outlineVariant;\n'
            '    final width = MediaQuery.sizeOf(context).width;\n'
            '    final expandedRail = width >= 1280;\n'
            '    final studentPaneWidth = width < 900 ? 288.0 : 320.0;\n'
            '    return Scaffold(\n'
            '      body: SafeArea(\n'
            '        child: Row(\n'
            '          children: [\n'
            '            SizedBox(\n'
            '              width: expandedRail ? 132 : 72,\n'
            '              child: _NavigationRail(\n'
            '                selectedIndex: destination,\n'
            '                expanded: expandedRail,\n'
            '                onSelected: onDestinationChanged,\n'
            '                onRefresh: onRefresh,\n'
            '                refreshing: refreshing,\n'
            '                onManage: onManage,\n'
            '                onSettings: onSettings,\n'
            '              ),\n'
            '            ),\n'
            '            VerticalDivider(width: 1, color: border),\n'
            '            if (destination == 1) ...[\n'
            '              SizedBox(\n'
            '                width: studentPaneWidth,\n'
            '                child: _StudentListPane(\n'
            '                  selectedStudent: selectedStudent,\n'
            '                  onSelected: onStudentSelected,\n'
            '                ),\n'
            '              ),\n'
            '              VerticalDivider(width: 1, color: border),\n'
            '              Expanded(\n'
            '                child: _QuietPaneTransition(\n'
            '                  transitionKey: showCase && selectedCase != null\n'
            '                      ? \'case-${selectedCase!.id}\'\n'
            '                      : \'student-${selectedStudent.id}\',\n'
            '                  child: showCase && selectedCase != null\n'
            '                      ? _CaseDetailPane(\n'
            '                          student: selectedStudent,\n'
            '                          item: selectedCase!,\n'
            '                          onBack: onBackFromCase,\n'
            '                        )\n'
            '                      : _StudentDetailPane(\n'
            '                          student: selectedStudent,\n'
            '                          onOpenCase: onOpenCase,\n'
            '                        ),\n'
            '                ),\n'
            '              ),\n'
            '            ] else if (destination == 0) ...[\n'
            '              Expanded(\n'
            '                child: _TodayPane(\n'
            '                  onOpenCase: onOpenCase,\n'
            '                  onOpenStudent: (student) {\n'
            '                    onStudentSelected(student);\n'
            '                    onDestinationChanged(1);\n'
            '                  },\n'
            '                ),\n'
            '              ),\n'
            '            ] else ...[\n'
            '              Expanded(child: _CaseIndexPane(onOpenCase: onOpenCase)),\n'
            '            ],\n'
            '          ],\n'
            '        ),\n'
            '      ),\n'
            '    );\n'
            '  }\n'
            '}\n'
            '\n'
            'class _CompactWorkspace extends StatefulWidget {\n'
            '  const _CompactWorkspace({\n'
            '    required this.destination,\n'
            '    required this.selectedStudent,\n'
            '    required this.selectedCase,\n'
            '    required this.showCase,\n'
            '    required this.onDestinationChanged,\n'
            '    required this.onStudentSelected,\n'
            '    required this.onOpenCase,\n'
            '    required this.onBackFromCase,\n'
            '    required this.onOpenMore,\n'
            '  });\n'
            '\n'
            '  final int destination;\n'
            '  final V2Student selectedStudent;\n'
            '  final V2FocusItem? selectedCase;\n'
            '  final bool showCase;\n'
            '  final ValueChanged<int> onDestinationChanged;\n'
            '  final ValueChanged<V2Student> onStudentSelected;\n'
            '  final ValueChanged<V2FocusItem> onOpenCase;\n'
            '  final VoidCallback onBackFromCase;\n'
            '  final VoidCallback? onOpenMore;\n'
            '\n'
            '  @override\n'
            '  State<_CompactWorkspace> createState() => _CompactWorkspaceState();\n'
            '}\n'
            '\n'
            'class _CompactWorkspaceState extends State<_CompactWorkspace> {\n'
            '  bool _studentOpen = false;\n'
            '\n'
            '  @override\n'
            '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
            '    Widget body;\n'
            '    if (widget.showCase && widget.selectedCase != null) {\n'
            '      body = _CaseDetailPane(\n'
            '        student: widget.selectedStudent,\n'
            '        item: widget.selectedCase!,\n'
            '        onBack: widget.onBackFromCase,\n'
            '        compact: true,\n'
            '      );\n'
            '    } else if (widget.destination == 1 && _studentOpen) {\n'
            '      body = _StudentDetailPane(\n'
            '        student: widget.selectedStudent,\n'
            '        onOpenCase: widget.onOpenCase,\n'
            '        compact: true,\n'
            '        onBack: () => setState(() => _studentOpen = false),\n'
            '      );\n'
            '    } else if (widget.destination == 1) {\n'
            '      body = _StudentListPane(\n'
            '        selectedStudent: widget.selectedStudent,\n'
            '        compact: true,\n'
            '        onOpenMore: widget.onOpenMore,\n'
            '        onSelected: (student) {\n'
            '          widget.onStudentSelected(student);\n'
            '          setState(() => _studentOpen = true);\n'
            '        },\n'
            '      );\n'
            '    } else if (widget.destination == 0) {\n'
            '      body = _TodayPane(\n'
            '        onOpenCase: widget.onOpenCase,\n'
            '        onOpenStudent: (student) {\n'
            '          widget.onStudentSelected(student);\n'
            '          widget.onDestinationChanged(1);\n'
            '          setState(() => _studentOpen = true);\n'
            '        },\n'
            '        compact: true,\n'
            '        onOpenMore: widget.onOpenMore,\n'
            '      );\n'
            '    } else {\n'
            '      body = _CaseIndexPane(\n'
            '        onOpenCase: widget.onOpenCase,\n'
            '        compact: true,\n'
            '        onOpenMore: widget.onOpenMore,\n'
            '      );\n'
            '    }\n'
            '\n'
            '    final hasInternalHistory = widget.showCase || _studentOpen;\n'
            '    final handlesSystemBack = hasInternalHistory || widget.destination != 0;\n'
            '    return PopScope<void>(\n'
            '      canPop: !handlesSystemBack,\n'
            '      onPopInvokedWithResult: (didPop, _) {\n'
            '        if (didPop) return;\n'
            '        if (widget.showCase) {\n'
            '          widget.onBackFromCase();\n'
            '          return;\n'
            '        }\n'
            '        if (_studentOpen) {\n'
            '          setState(() => _studentOpen = false);\n'
            '          return;\n'
            '        }\n'
            '        if (widget.destination != 0) {\n'
            '          widget.onDestinationChanged(0);\n'
            '        }\n'
            '      },\n'
            '      child: Scaffold(\n'
            '        backgroundColor: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.surface,\n'
            '        body: SafeArea(child: body),\n'
            '        bottomNavigationBar: hasInternalHistory\n'
            '            ? null\n'
            '            : NavigationBar(\n'
            '                backgroundColor: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.surface,\n'
            '                selectedIndex: widget.destination,\n'
            '                onDestinationSelected: (value) {\n'
            '                  setState(() => _studentOpen = false);\n'
            '                  widget.onDestinationChanged(value);\n'
            '                },\n'
            '                destinations: const [\n'
            '                  NavigationDestination(\n'
            '                    icon: Icon(Icons.today_outlined),\n'
            '                    selectedIcon: Icon(Icons.today),\n'
            '                    label: \'今日\',\n'
            '                  ),\n'
            '                  NavigationDestination(\n'
            '                    icon: Icon(Icons.people_outline),\n'
            '                    selectedIcon: Icon(Icons.people),\n'
            '                    label: \'学生\',\n'
            '                  ),\n'
            '                  NavigationDestination(\n'
            '                    icon: Icon(Icons.fact_check_outlined),\n'
            '                    selectedIcon: Icon(Icons.fact_check),\n'
            '                    label: \'学情\',\n'
            '                  ),\n'
            '                ],\n'
            '              ),\n'
            '      ),\n'
            '    );\n'
            '  }\n'
            '}\n'
            '\n'
            'class _NavigationRail extends StatelessWidget {\n'
            '  const _NavigationRail({\n'
            '    required this.selectedIndex,\n'
            '    required this.onSelected,\n'
            '    required this.onSettings,\n'
            '    required this.refreshing,\n'
            '    this.expanded = false,\n'
            '    this.onRefresh,\n'
            '    this.onManage,\n'
            '  });\n'
            '\n'
            '  final int selectedIndex;\n'
            '  final bool expanded;\n'
            '  final ValueChanged<int> onSelected;\n'
            '  final VoidCallback? onRefresh;\n'
            '  final bool refreshing;\n'
            '  final VoidCallback? onManage;\n'
            '  final VoidCallback onSettings;\n'
            '\n'
            '  @override\n'
            '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
            '    final scheme = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;\n'
            '    return ColoredBox(\n'
            '      color: scheme.surfaceContainerLowest,\n'
            '      child: Column(\n'
            '        children: [\n'
            '          const SizedBox(height: 18),\n'
            '          Text(\n'
            '            \'学情\',\n'
            '            style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.labelMedium?.copyWith(\n'
            '              color: scheme.primary,\n'
            '              fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,\n'
            '              letterSpacing: 0.6,\n'
            '            ),\n'
            '          ),\n'
            '          const SizedBox(height: 22),\n'
            '          for (final item in const [\n'
            '            (Icons.today_outlined, \'今日\'),\n'
            '            (Icons.people_outline, \'学生\'),\n'
            '            (Icons.fact_check_outlined, \'学情\'),\n'
            '          ].indexed)\n'
            '            _RailItem(\n'
            '              icon: item.$2.$1,\n'
            '              tooltip: item.$2.$2,\n'
            '              selected: selectedIndex == item.$1,\n'
            '              onTap: () => onSelected(item.$1),\n'
            '              expanded: expanded,\n'
            '            ),\n'
            '          const Spacer(),\n'
            '          if (onRefresh != null)\n'
            '            _RailItem(\n'
            '              key: const Key(\'v2-workspace-refresh\'),\n'
            '              icon: Icons.refresh_outlined,\n'
            '              tooltip: refreshing ? \'正在刷新\' : \'刷新学情\',\n'
            '              onTap: refreshing ? null : onRefresh,\n'
            '              expanded: expanded,\n'
            '            ),\n'
            '          if (onManage != null)\n'
            '            _RailItem(\n'
            '              icon: Icons.admin_panel_settings_outlined,\n'
            '              tooltip: \'管理\',\n'
            '              onTap: onManage,\n'
            '              expanded: expanded,\n'
            '            ),\n'
            '          _RailItem(\n'
            '            icon: Icons.settings_outlined,\n'
            '            tooltip: \'设置\',\n'
            '            onTap: onSettings,\n'
            '            expanded: expanded,\n'
            '          ),\n'
            '          const SizedBox(height: 12),\n'
            '        ],\n'
            '      ),\n'
            '    );\n'
            '  }\n'
            '}\n'
            '\n'
            'class _RailItem extends StatelessWidget {\n'
            '  const _RailItem({\n'
            '    super.key,\n'
            '    required this.icon,\n'
            '    required this.tooltip,\n'
            '    this.selected = false,\n'
            '    this.expanded = false,\n'
            '    this.onTap,\n'
            '  });\n'
            '\n'
            '  final IconData icon;\n'
            '  final String tooltip;\n'
            '  final bool selected;\n'
            '  final bool expanded;\n'
            '  final VoidCallback? onTap;\n'
            '\n'
            '  @override\n'
            '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
            '    final scheme = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;\n'
            '    final foreground = selected\n'
            '        ? scheme.onPrimaryContainer\n'
            '        : scheme.onSurfaceVariant;\n'
            '    return Padding(\n'
            '      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 3, horizontal: 10),\n'
            '      child: Tooltip(\n'
            '        message: tooltip,\n'
            '        child: AnimatedContainer(\n'
            '          duration: AppMotion.effectiveDuration(context, AppMotion.quick),\n'
            '          curve: AppMotion.enter,\n'
            '          decoration: BoxDecoration(\n'
            '            color: selected ? scheme.primaryContainer : Colors.transparent,\n'
            '            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(9),\n'
            '          ),\n'
            '          child: Material(\n'
            '            type: MaterialType.transparency,\n'
            '            child: InkWell(\n'
            '              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(9),\n'
            '              onTap: onTap,\n'
            '              child: SizedBox(\n'
            '                width: expanded ? 108 : 48,\n'
            '                height: 48,\n'
            '                child: Row(\n'
            '                  mainAxisAlignment: expanded\n'
            '                      ? MainAxisAlignment.start\n'
            '                      : MainAxisAlignment.center,\n'
            '                  children: [\n'
            '                    if (expanded) const SizedBox(width: 12),\n'
            '                    Icon(icon, size: 20, color: foreground),\n'
            '                    if (expanded) ...[\n'
            '                      const SizedBox(width: 10),\n'
            '                      Expanded(\n'
            '                        child: Text(\n'
            '                          tooltip,\n'
            '                          maxLines: 1,\n'
            '                          overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,\n'
            '                          style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.labelMedium\n'
            '                              ?.copyWith(\n'
            '                                color: foreground,\n'
            '                                fontWeight: selected\n'
            '                                    ? FontWeight.w600\n'
            '                                    : FontWeight.w500,\n'
            '                              ),\n'
            '                        ),\n'
            '                      ),\n'
            '                      const SizedBox(width: 8),\n'
            '                    ],\n'
            '                  ],\n'
            '                ),\n'
            '              ),\n'
            '            ),\n'
            '          ),\n'
            '        ),\n'
            '      ),\n'
            '    );\n'
            '  }\n'
            '}\n'
            '\n'
            'class _StudentListPane extends StatefulWidget {\n'
            '  const _StudentListPane({\n'
            '    required this.selectedStudent,\n'
            '    required this.onSelected,\n'
            '    this.compact = false,\n'
            '    this.onOpenMore,\n'
            '  });\n'
            '\n'
            '  final V2Student selectedStudent;\n'
            '  final ValueChanged<V2Student> onSelected;\n'
            '  final bool compact;\n'
            '  final VoidCallback? onOpenMore;\n'
            '\n'
            '  @override\n'
            '  State<_StudentListPane> createState() => _StudentListPaneState();\n'
            '}\n'
            '\n'
            'class _StudentListPaneState extends State<_StudentListPane> {\n'
            '  final _searchController = TextEditingController();\n'
            '  String _query = \'\';\n'
            '\n'
            '  @override\n'
            '  void dispose() {\n'
            '    _searchController.dispose();\n'
            '    super.dispose();\n'
            '  }\n'
            '\n'
            '  List<V2Student> _visibleStudents(V2WorkspaceData data) {\n'
            '    final query = _query.trim().toLowerCase();\n'
            '    if (query.isEmpty) {\n'
            '      return data.students;\n'
            '    }\n'
            '    return data.students\n'
            '        .where((student) {\n'
            '          final haystack = [\n'
            '            student.name,\n'
            '            student.grade,\n'
            '            ...student.subjects,\n'
            '          ].join(\' \').toLowerCase();\n'
            '          return haystack.contains(query);\n'
            '        })\n'
            '        .toList(growable: false);\n'
            '  }\n'
            '\n'
            '  void _clearSearch() {\n'
            '    _searchController.clear();\n'
            '    setState(() => _query = \'\');\n'
            '  }\n'
            '\n'
            '  @override\n'
            '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
            '    final data = V2WorkspaceDataScope.of(context);\n'
            '    final visibleStudents = _visibleStudents(data);\n'
            '    final scheme = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;\n'
            '    return ColoredBox(\n'
            '      color: widget.compact ? scheme.surface : scheme.surfaceContainerLowest,\n'
            '      child: Column(\n'
            '        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
            '        children: [\n'
            '          Padding(\n'
            '            padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(\n'
            '              widget.compact ? 18 : 24,\n'
            '              24,\n'
            '              widget.compact ? 18 : 20,\n'
            '              12,\n'
            '            ),\n'
            '            child: Column(\n'
            '              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
            '              children: [\n'
            '                Row(\n'
            '                  children: [\n'
            '                    Expanded(\n'
            '                      child: Text(\n'
            '                        \'学生\',\n'
            '                        style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.headlineSmall,\n'
            '                      ),\n'
            '                    ),\n'
            '                    if (widget.compact && widget.onOpenMore != null)\n'
            '                      IconButton(\n'
            '                        key: const Key(\'v2-compact-more\'),\n'
            '                        tooltip: \'更多操作\',\n'
            '                        onPressed: widget.onOpenMore,\n'
            '                        icon: const Icon(Icons.more_vert),\n'
            '                      ),\n'
            '                  ],\n'
            '                ),\n'
            '                const SizedBox(height: 18),\n'
            '                TextField(\n'
            '                  key: const Key(\'v2-student-search\'),\n'
            '                  controller: _searchController,\n'
            '                  onChanged: (value) => setState(() => _query = value),\n'
            '                  decoration: InputDecoration(\n'
            '                    hintText: \'搜索姓名、年级或学科…\',\n'
            '                    prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.search, size: 19),\n'
            '                    suffixIcon: _query.isEmpty\n'
            '                        ? null\n'
            '                        : IconButton(\n'
            '                            tooltip: \'清除搜索\',\n'
            '                            onPressed: _clearSearch,\n'
            '                            icon: const Icon(Icons.close, size: 18),\n'
            '                          ),\n'
            '                  ),\n'
            '                ),\n'
            '                const SizedBox(height: 14),\n'
            '                Text(\n'
            '                  _query.trim().isEmpty\n'
            '                      ? \'全部 ${data.students.length}\'\n'
            '                      : \'找到 ${visibleStudents.length} 位\',\n'
            '                  style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.labelLarge,\n'
            '                ),\n'
            '              ],\n'
            '            ),\n'
            '          ),\n'
            '          Divider(\n'
            '            height: 1,\n'
            '            color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.outlineVariant,\n'
            '          ),\n'
            '          Expanded(\n'
            '            child: visibleStudents.isEmpty\n'
            '                ? Center(\n'
            '                    child: Text(\n'
            '                      \'没有找到匹配的学生\',\n'
            '                      style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium,\n'
            '                    ),\n'
            '                  )\n'
            '                : ListView.builder(\n'
            '                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 4),\n'
            '                    itemCount: visibleStudents.length,\n'
            '                    itemBuilder: (context, index) {\n'
            '                      final student = visibleStudents[index];\n'
            '                      return _StudentRow(\n'
            '                        student: student,\n'
            '                        selected:\n'
            '                            !widget.compact &&\n'
            '                            student.id == widget.selectedStudent.id,\n'
            '                        onTap: () => widget.onSelected(student),\n'
            '                      );\n'
            '                    },\n'
            '                  ),\n'
            '          ),\n'
            '        ],\n'
            '      ),\n'
            '    );\n'
            '  }\n'
            '}\n'
            '\n'
            'class _StudentRow extends StatelessWidget {\n'
            '  const _StudentRow({\n'
            '    required this.student,\n'
            '    required this.selected,\n'
            '    required this.onTap,\n'
            '  });\n'
            '\n'
            '  final V2Student student;\n'
            '  final bool selected;\n'
            '  final VoidCallback onTap;\n'
            '\n'
            '  @override\n'
            '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
            '    final scheme = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;\n'
            '    return Padding(\n'
            '      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 2),\n'
            '      child: AnimatedContainer(\n'
            '        duration: AppMotion.effectiveDuration(context, AppMotion.quick),\n'
            '        curve: AppMotion.enter,\n'
            '        decoration: BoxDecoration(\n'
            '          color: selected\n'
            '              ? scheme.primary.withValues(alpha: 0.07)\n'
            '              : Colors.transparent,\n'
            '          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(9),\n'
            '        ),\n'
            '        child: Material(\n'
            '          type: MaterialType.transparency,\n'
            '          child: InkWell(\n'
            '            onTap: onTap,\n'
            '            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(9),\n'
            '            child: Padding(\n'
            '              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14, vertical: 12),\n'
            '              child: Row(\n'
            '                children: [\n'
            '                  _InitialMark(name: student.name),\n'
            '                  const SizedBox(width: 12),\n'
            '                  Expanded(\n'
            '                    child: Column(\n'
            '                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
            '                      children: [\n'
            '                        Text(\n'
            '                          student.name,\n'
            '                          style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleMedium,\n'
            '                        ),\n'
            '                        const SizedBox(height: 2),\n'
            '                        Text(\n'
            '                          \'${student.grade} · ${student.subjects.join(\' / \')}\',\n'
            '                          style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall,\n'
            '                        ),\n'
            '                      ],\n'
            '                    ),\n'
            '                  ),\n'
            '                  const SizedBox(width: 10),\n'
            '                  Column(\n'
            '                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end,\n'
            '                    children: [\n'
            '                      Text(\n'
            '                        student.openCaseCount == 0\n'
            '                            ? \'暂无进行中\'\n'
            '                            : \'${student.openCaseCount} 个进行中\',\n'
            '                        style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall,\n'
            '                      ),\n'
            '                      if (student.updatedLabel != \'暂无记录\') ...[\n'
            '                        const SizedBox(height: 3),\n'
            '                        Text(\n'
            '                          student.updatedLabel,\n'
            '                          style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall\n'
            '                              ?.copyWith(\n'
            '                                color: scheme.onSurfaceVariant.withValues(\n'
            '                                  alpha: 0.72,\n'
            '                                ),\n'
            '                              ),\n'
            '                        ),\n'
            '                      ],\n'
            '                    ],\n'
            '                  ),\n'
            '                ],\n'
            '              ),\n'
            '            ),\n'
            '          ),\n'
            '        ),\n'
            '      ),\n'
            '    );\n'
            '  }\n'
            '}\n'
            '\n'
            'class _InitialMark extends StatelessWidget {\n'
            '  const _InitialMark({required this.name});\n'
            '\n'
            '  final String name;\n'
            '\n'
            '  @override\n'
            '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
            '    final scheme = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;\n'
            '    return Container(\n'
            '      width: 34,\n'
            '      height: 34,\n'
            '      alignment: Alignment.center,\n'
            '      decoration: BoxDecoration(\n'
            '        color: scheme.surfaceContainer,\n'
            '        shape: BoxShape.circle,\n'
            '      ),\n'
            '      child: Text(\n'
            '        name.characters.first,\n'
            '        style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.labelLarge,\n'
            '      ),\n'
            '    );\n'
            '  }\n'
            '}\n'
            '\n'
            'class _StudentDetailPane extends StatefulWidget {\n'
            '  const _StudentDetailPane({\n'
            '    required this.student,\n'
            '    required this.onOpenCase,\n'
            '    this.compact = false,\n'
            '    this.onBack,\n'
            '  });\n'
            '\n'
            '  final V2Student student;\n'
            '  final ValueChanged<V2FocusItem> onOpenCase;\n'
            '  final bool compact;\n'
            '  final VoidCallback? onBack;\n'
            '\n'
            '  @override\n'
            '  State<_StudentDetailPane> createState() => _StudentDetailPaneState();\n'
            '}\n'
            '\n'
            'class _StudentDetailPaneState extends State<_StudentDetailPane> {\n'
            '  bool _showAllFocusItems = false;\n'
            '\n'
            '  @override\n'
            '  void didUpdateWidget(covariant _StudentDetailPane oldWidget) {\n'
            '    super.didUpdateWidget(oldWidget);\n'
            '    if (oldWidget.student.id != widget.student.id) {\n'
            '      _showAllFocusItems = false;\n'
            '    }\n'
            '  }\n'
            '\n'
            '  @override\n'
            '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
            '    final scheme = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;\n'
            '    final data = V2WorkspaceDataScope.of(context);\n'
            '    final focusItems = data.focusItemsForStudent(widget.student);\n'
            '    final visibleFocusItems = _showAllFocusItems\n'
            '        ? focusItems\n'
            '        : focusItems.take(_studentFocusPreviewLimit).toList(growable: false);\n'
            '    final hasAdditionalFocusItems =\n'
            '        focusItems.length > _studentFocusPreviewLimit;\n'
            '    final closedItems = data.closedItemsForStudent(widget.student);\n'
            '    final timelineEntries = data.timelineForStudent(widget.student);\n'
            '    return ColoredBox(\n'
            '      color: scheme.surface,\n'
            '      child: CustomScrollView(\n'
            '        slivers: [\n'
            '          SliverToBoxAdapter(\n'
            '            child: Center(\n'
            '              child: ConstrainedBox(\n'
            '                constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 900),\n'
            '                child: Padding(\n'
            '                  padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(\n'
            '                    widget.compact ? 18 : 32,\n'
            '                    22,\n'
            '                    widget.compact ? 18 : 32,\n'
            '                    48,\n'
            '                  ),\n'
            '                  child: Column(\n'
            '                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
            '                    children: [\n'
            '                      if (widget.onBack != null) ...[\n'
            '                        IconButton(\n'
            '                          tooltip: \'返回学生列表\',\n'
            '                          onPressed: widget.onBack,\n'
            '                          icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back),\n'
            '                        ),\n'
            '                        const SizedBox(height: 4),\n'
            '                      ],\n'
            '                      _StudentHeader(\n'
            '                        student: widget.student,\n'
            '                        compact: widget.compact,\n'
            '                      ),\n'
            '                      const SizedBox(height: 30),\n'
            '                      _SectionTitle(\n'
            '                        title: _showAllFocusItems ? \'全部问题\' : \'现在最重要\',\n'
            '                        count: visibleFocusItems.length,\n'
            '                      ),\n'
            '                      const SizedBox(height: 8),\n'
            '                      if (focusItems.isEmpty)\n'
            '                        Padding(\n'
            '                          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 18),\n'
            '                          child: Text(\n'
            '                            \'暂无进行中的问题\',\n'
            '                            style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium,\n'
            '                          ),\n'
            '                        )\n'
            '                      else ...[\n'
            '                        for (var i = 0; i < visibleFocusItems.length; i++) ...[\n'
            '                          _FocusRow(\n'
            '                            item: visibleFocusItems[i],\n'
            '                            onTap: () =>\n'
            '                                widget.onOpenCase(visibleFocusItems[i]),\n'
            '                          ),\n'
            '                          if (i < visibleFocusItems.length - 1)\n'
            '                            Divider(height: 1, color: scheme.outlineVariant),\n'
            '                        ],\n'
            '                        if (hasAdditionalFocusItems) ...[\n'
            '                          const SizedBox(height: 6),\n'
            '                          TextButton.icon(\n'
            '                            key: const Key(\'v2-student-focus-toggle\'),\n'
            '                            onPressed: () => setState(\n'
            '                              () => _showAllFocusItems = !_showAllFocusItems,\n'
            '                            ),\n'
            '                            icon: Icon(\n'
            '                              _showAllFocusItems\n'
            '                                  ? Icons.expand_less\n'
            '                                  : Icons.expand_more,\n'
            '                              size: 18,\n'
            '                            ),\n'
            '                            label: Text(\n'
            '                              _showAllFocusItems\n'
            '                                  ? \'只看重点\'\n'
            '                                  : \'查看全部 ${focusItems.length} 个\',\n'
            '                            ),\n'
            '                          ),\n'
            '                        ],\n'
            '                      ],\n'
            '                      if (closedItems.isNotEmpty) ...[\n'
            '                        const SizedBox(height: 34),\n'
            '                        _SectionTitle(title: \'历史问题\', count: closedItems.length),\n'
            '                        const SizedBox(height: 8),\n'
            '                        for (var i = 0; i < closedItems.length; i++) ...[\n'
            '                          _FocusRow(\n'
            '                            item: closedItems[i],\n'
            '                            onTap: () => widget.onOpenCase(closedItems[i]),\n'
            '                          ),\n'
            '                          if (i < closedItems.length - 1)\n'
            '                            Divider(height: 1, color: scheme.outlineVariant),\n'
            '                        ],\n'
            '                      ],\n'
            '                      const SizedBox(height: 34),\n'
            '                      const _SectionTitle(title: \'最近成长\'),\n'
            '                      const SizedBox(height: 14),\n'
            '                      _Timeline(entries: timelineEntries),\n'
            '                    ],\n'
            '                  ),\n'
            '                ),\n'
            '              ),\n'
            '            ),\n'
            '          ),\n'
            '        ],\n'
            '      ),\n'
            '    );\n'
            '  }\n'
            '}\n'
            '\n'
            'class _StudentHeader extends StatelessWidget {\n'
            '  const _StudentHeader({required this.student, required this.compact});\n'
            '\n'
            '  final V2Student student;\n'
            '  final bool compact;\n'
            '\n'
            '  @override\n'
            '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
            '    final data = V2WorkspaceDataScope.of(context);\n'
            '    final focusItems = data.focusItemsForStudent(student);\n'
            '    final exportStudent = _V2RuntimeScope.maybeOf(context)?.studentExport;\n'
            '    final buttons = Wrap(\n'
            '      spacing: 8,\n'
            '      runSpacing: 8,\n'
            '      children: [\n'
            '        OutlinedButton.icon(\n'
            '          onPressed: () => _showV2QuickCaptureForStudent(context, student),\n'
            '          icon: const Icon(Icons.note_add_outlined, size: 18),\n'
            '          label: const Text(\'记录问题\'),\n'
            '        ),\n'
            '        FilledButton.icon(\n'
            '          onPressed: focusItems.isEmpty\n'
            '              ? null\n'
            '              : () => _showV2ProgressCasePicker(context, student),\n'
            '          icon: const Icon(Icons.edit_note_outlined, size: 18),\n'
            '          label: const Text(\'记进展\'),\n'
            '        ),\n'
            '        if (exportStudent != null)\n'
            '          PopupMenuButton<String>(\n'
            '            key: const Key(\'v2-student-more-actions\'),\n'
            '            tooltip: \'更多操作\',\n'
            '            icon: const Icon(Icons.more_horiz),\n'
            '            onSelected: (value) async {\n'
            '              if (value == \'export\') {\n'
            '                await exportStudent(context, student);\n'
            '              }\n'
            '            },\n'
            '            itemBuilder: (_) => const [\n'
            '              PopupMenuItem<String>(\n'
            '                value: \'export\',\n'
            '                child: ListTile(\n'
            '                  leading: Icon(Icons.download_outlined),\n'
            '                  title: Text(\'导出学情记录\'),\n'
            '                  contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,\n'
            '                ),\n'
            '              ),\n'
            '            ],\n'
            '          ),\n'
            '      ],\n'
            '    );\n'
            '    return Column(\n'
            '      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
            '      children: [\n'
            '        Text(\n'
            '          \'学生 · ${student.name}\',\n'
            '          style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall,\n'
            '        ),\n'
            '        const SizedBox(height: 18),\n'
            '        if (compact) ...[\n'
            '          Text(student.name, style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.headlineSmall),\n'
            '          const SizedBox(height: 5),\n'
            '          Text(\n'
            '            \'${student.grade} · ${student.subjects.join(\' / \')}\',\n'
            '            style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyLarge,\n'
            '          ),\n'
            '          if (student.teacherSummary.trim().isNotEmpty) ...[\n'
            '            const SizedBox(height: 5),\n'
            '            Text(\n'
            '              student.teacherSummary,\n'
            '              style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall,\n'
            '            ),\n'
            '          ],\n'
            '          const SizedBox(height: 16),\n'
            '          buttons,\n'
            '        ] else\n'
            '          Row(\n'
            '            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
            '            children: [\n'
            '              Expanded(\n'
            '                child: Column(\n'
            '                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
            '                  children: [\n'
            '                    Text(\n'
            '                      student.name,\n'
            '                      style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.headlineSmall,\n'
            '                    ),\n'
            '                    const SizedBox(height: 5),\n'
            '                    Text(\n'
            '                      \'${student.grade} · ${student.subjects.join(\' / \')}\',\n'
            '                      style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyLarge,\n'
            '                    ),\n'
            '                    if (student.teacherSummary.trim().isNotEmpty) ...[\n'
            '                      const SizedBox(height: 5),\n'
            '                      Text(\n'
            '                        student.teacherSummary,\n'
            '                        style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall,\n'
            '                      ),\n'
            '                    ],\n'
            '                  ],\n'
            '                ),\n'
            '              ),\n'
            '              buttons,\n'
            '            ],\n'
            '          ),\n'
            '      ],\n'
            '    );\n'
            '  }\n'
            '}\n'
            '\n'
            'class _SectionTitle extends StatelessWidget {\n'
            '  const _SectionTitle({required this.title, this.count});\n'
            '\n'
            '  final String title;\n'
            '  final int? count;\n'
            '\n'
            '  @override\n'
            '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
            '    return Row(\n'
            '      children: [\n'
            '        Text(title, style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleLarge),\n'
            '        if (count != null) ...[\n'
            '          const SizedBox(width: 8),\n'
            '          Text(\'$count\', style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall),\n'
            '        ],\n'
            '      ],\n'
            '    );\n'
            '  }\n'
            '}\n'
            '\n'
            'class _FocusRow extends StatelessWidget {\n'
            '  const _FocusRow({required this.item, required this.onTap, this.studentName});\n'
            '\n'
            '  final V2FocusItem item;\n'
            '  final VoidCallback onTap;\n'
            '  final String? studentName;\n'
            '\n'
            '  @override\n'
            '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
            '    final scheme = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;\n'
            '    return InkWell(\n'
            '      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),\n'
            '      onTap: onTap,\n'
            '      child: Padding(\n'
            '        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16),\n'
            '        child: Row(\n'
            '          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
            '          children: [\n'
            '            Container(\n'
            '              width: 2,\n'
            '              height: 58,\n'
            '              color: scheme.primary.withValues(alpha: 0.5),\n'
            '            ),\n'
            '            const SizedBox(width: 16),\n'
            '            Expanded(\n'
            '              child: Column(\n'
            '                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
            '                children: [\n'
            '                  Text(\n'
            '                    item.title,\n'
            '                    maxLines: 2,\n'
            '                    overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,\n'
            '                    style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleMedium,\n'
            '                  ),\n'
            '                  if (studentName != null) ...[\n'
            '                    const SizedBox(height: 4),\n'
            '                    Text(\n'
            '                      \'$studentName · ${item.subject}\',\n'
            '                      style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall,\n'
            '                    ),\n'
            '                  ],\n'
            '                  if (studentName == null && _shouldShowCaseSummary(item)) ...[\n'
            '                    const SizedBox(height: 4),\n'
            '                    Text(\n'
            '                      item.summary,\n'
            '                      style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium,\n'
            '                    ),\n'
            '                  ],\n'
            '                  const SizedBox(height: 8),\n'
            '                  Text(\n'
            '                    item.closed\n'
            '                        ? _displayNextStep(item.nextStep)\n'
            '                        : \'下一步  ${_displayNextStep(item.nextStep)}\',\n'
            '                    maxLines: 2,\n'
            '                    overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,\n'
            '                    style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall,\n'
            '                  ),\n'
            '                ],\n'
            '              ),\n'
            '            ),\n'
            '            if (studentName == null) ...[\n'
            '              const SizedBox(width: 16),\n'
            '              Column(\n'
            '                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end,\n'
            '                children: [\n'
            '                  Text(\n'
            '                    item.subject,\n'
            '                    style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall,\n'
            '                  ),\n'
            '                  const SizedBox(height: 17),\n'
            '                  Text(\n'
            '                    item.dueLabel,\n'
            '                    style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall,\n'
            '                  ),\n'
            '                ],\n'
            '              ),\n'
            '            ] else if (!item.closed &&\n'
            '                item.dueLabel.trim().isNotEmpty &&\n'
            '                item.dueLabel != \'待安排\') ...[\n'
            '              const SizedBox(width: 12),\n'
            '              Text(item.dueLabel, style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall),\n'
            '            ],\n'
            '            const SizedBox(width: 4),\n'
            '            const Icon(Icons.chevron_right, size: 20),\n'
            '          ],\n'
            '        ),\n'
            '      ),\n'
            '    );\n'
            '  }\n'
            '}\n'
            '\n'
            'class _Timeline extends StatelessWidget {\n'
            '  const _Timeline({required this.entries, this.resolveEvidencePhotos = false});\n'
            '\n'
            '  final List<V2TimelineEntry> entries;\n'
            '  final bool resolveEvidencePhotos;\n'
            '\n'
            '  @override\n'
            '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
            '    if (entries.isEmpty) {\n'
            '      return Text(\'暂时还没有成长记录\', style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium);\n'
            '    }\n'
            '    return Column(\n'
            '      children: [\n'
            '        for (var i = 0; i < entries.length; i++)\n'
            '          _TimelineRow(\n'
            '            entry: entries[i],\n'
            '            last: i == entries.length - 1,\n'
            '            resolveEvidencePhotos: resolveEvidencePhotos,\n'
            '          ),\n'
            '      ],\n'
            '    );\n'
            '  }\n'
            '}\n'
            '\n'
            'class _TimelineRow extends StatelessWidget {\n'
            '  const _TimelineRow({\n'
            '    required this.entry,\n'
            '    required this.last,\n'
            '    required this.resolveEvidencePhotos,\n'
            '  });\n'
            '\n'
            '  final V2TimelineEntry entry;\n'
            '  final bool last;\n'
            '  final bool resolveEvidencePhotos;\n'
            '\n'
            '  @override\n'
            '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
            '    final scheme = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;\n'
            '    final attachmentRepository = _V2RuntimeScope.maybeOf(context)\n'
            '        ?.evidenceAttachmentRepository;\n'
            '    final canResolveRealPhotos =\n'
            '        resolveEvidencePhotos &&\n'
            '        entry.evidenceId != null &&\n'
            '        attachmentRepository != null;\n'
            '    return Row(\n'
            '      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
            '      children: [\n'
            '        SizedBox(\n'
            '          width: 74,\n'
            '          child: Padding(\n'
            '            padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 1),\n'
            '            child: Text(\n'
            '              entry.date,\n'
            '              style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall,\n'
            '            ),\n'
            '          ),\n'
            '        ),\n'
            '        SizedBox(\n'
            '          width: 24,\n'
            '          child: Column(\n'
            '            children: [\n'
            '              Container(\n'
            '                width: 7,\n'
            '                height: 7,\n'
            '                margin: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 7),\n'
            '                decoration: BoxDecoration(\n'
            '                  color: entry.kind == \'新表现\' ? scheme.primary : scheme.outline,\n'
            '                  shape: BoxShape.circle,\n'
            '                ),\n'
            '              ),\n'
            '              if (!last)\n'
            '                Container(\n'
            '                  width: 1,\n'
            '                  height: entry.photoCount > 0 || canResolveRealPhotos\n'
            '                      ? 144\n'
            '                      : 96,\n'
            '                  color: scheme.outlineVariant,\n'
            '                ),\n'
            '            ],\n'
            '          ),\n'
            '        ),\n'
            '        const SizedBox(width: 10),\n'
            '        Expanded(\n'
            '          child: Padding(\n'
            '            padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 24),\n'
            '            child: Column(\n'
            '              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,\n'
            '              children: [\n'
            '                Text(\n'
            '                  entry.kind,\n'
            '                  style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleMedium,\n'
            '                ),\n'
            '                const SizedBox(height: 5),\n'
            '                Text(entry.body, style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium),\n'
            '                if (canResolveRealPhotos) ...[\n'
            '                  const SizedBox(height: 12),\n'
            '                  _EvidencePhotoStrip(\n'
            '                    evidenceId: entry.evidenceId!,\n'
            '                    repository: attachmentRepository,\n'
            '                  ),\n'
            '                ] else if (entry.photoCount > 0) ...[\n'
            '                  const SizedBox(height: 12),\n'
            '                  _PhotoStrip(count: entry.photoCount),\n'
            '                ],\n'
            '                const SizedBox(height: 9),\n'
            '                Text(\n'
            '                  entry.teacher.trim().isEmpty\n'
            '                      ? entry.time\n'
            '                      : \'${entry.teacher} · ${entry.time}\',\n'
